def $min_(i, j)

1. If ((i <= j)), then

  1. Return i

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return j

def $lsl_(int_1, int_2)

1. If ((int_2 > (0 as int))), then

  1. Return $lsl_((int_1 * (2 as int)), (int_2 - (1 as int)))

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return int_1

def $rev_<X>((X)*{X <- X*})

1. Case analysis on (X)*{X <- X*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return []

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let X_h :: (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} be (X)*{X <- X*})

      1. Return $rev_<X>((X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*}) ++ [X_h]

def $make_<X>(X, n)

1. If ((n = 0)), then

  1. Return []

1. Else Dangling#10

2. If ((n > 0)), then

  1. (Let int be (n - 1))

    1. If ((int has type n)), then

      1. (Let n' be (int as n))

        1. Return $make_<X>(X, n') ++ [X]

    1. Else Dangling#14

2. Else Dangling#12

def $drop_<X>((X)*{X <- X*}, n)

1. If ((n = 0)), then

  1. Return (X)*{X <- X*}

1. Else Dangling#17

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. If ((n > 0)), then

      1. Return []

    1. Else Dangling#20

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let X_h :: (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} be (X)*{X <- X*})

      1. If ((n > 0)), then

        1. (Let int be (n - 1))

          1. If ((int has type n)), then

            1. (Let n' be (int as n))

              1. Return $drop_<X>((X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*}, n')

          1. Else Dangling#25

      1. Else Dangling#23

      1. Else Dangling#34

def $replace_slice_<X>((X)*{X <- X*}, n, (X_r)*{X_r <- X_r*})

  1. Return (X_r)*{X_r <- X_r*} ++ $drop_<X>((X)*{X <- X*}, |(X_r)*{X_r <- X_r*}|)

1. Else Dangling#39

  1. (Let X_h :: (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} be (X)*{X <- X*})

        1. Else Dangling#45

    1. Else Dangling#43

2. Else Dangling#41

def $list_exists<X>($p(X) : bool, (X)*{X <- X*})

1. Case analysis on (X)*{X <- X*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return false

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let X_h :: (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} be (X)*{X <- X*})

      1. Case analysis on $p(X_h)

        1. Case true

          1. Return true

        2. Case false

          1. Return $list_exists<X>($p, (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*})

syntax set<K> = 
   | `{ K* } (from set<K>) 

def $empty_set<K>

1. Return (`{ [] })

builtin def $union_set<K>(set<K>, set<K>')

builtin def $diff_set<K>(set<K>, set<K>')

def $in_set<K>(K, (`{ (K')*{K' <- K'*} }))

1. Return K is in (K')*{K' <- K'*}

def $map_set<X, Y>($f(X) : Y, set<X>)

1. If ((set<X> = (`{ [] }))), then

  1. Return (`{ [] })

1. Else Dangling#56

2. (Let (`{ (X)*{X <- X*} }) be set<X>)

  1. If (((X)*{X <- X*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

    1. (Let X_h :: (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} be (X)*{X <- X*})

      1. Return $union_set<Y>((`{ [$f(X_h)] }), $map_set<X, Y>($f, (`{ (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} })))

  1. Else Dangling#59

def $min_i64

1. Return (-9223372036854775808 as i64)

def $le_u(i64_1, i64_2)

1. (Let i64_1' be (i64_1 + $min_i64))

  1. (Let i64_2' be (i64_2 + $min_i64))

    1. If ((i64_1' <= i64_2')), then

      1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $lt_u(i64_1, i64_2)

1. (Let i64_1' be (i64_1 + $min_i64))

  1. (Let i64_2' be (i64_2 + $min_i64))

    1. If ((i64_1' < i64_2')), then

      1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $range(nat)

1. If ((nat = 0)), then

  1. Return []

1. Else Dangling#73

2. If ((nat > 0)), then

  1. (Let int be (nat - 1))

    1. If ((int has type n)), then

      1. (Let n be (int as n))

        1. Return $range(n) ++ [n]

    1. Else Dangling#77

2. Else Dangling#75

syntax null = 
   | NoNull (from null) 
   | Null (from null) 

syntax mut = 
   | Cons (from mut) 
   | Var (from mut) 

syntax init = 
   | Set (from init) 
   | Unset (from init) 

syntax final = 
   | Final (from final) 
   | NoFinal (from final) 

syntax limits = {MIN i64, MAX i64?}

syntax range = i64

syntax typevar = 
   | StatX typeidx (from typevar) 
   | RecX nat (from typevar) 

syntax typevarConcrete = 
   | StatX typeidx (from typevarConcrete) 

syntax addrtype = 
   | I32AT (from addrtype) 
   | I64AT (from addrtype) 

syntax numtype = 
   | I32T (from numtype) 
   | I64T (from numtype) 
   | F32T (from numtype) 
   | F64T (from numtype) 

syntax vectype = 
   | V128T (from vectype) 

syntax heaptypeVar = 
   | VarHT typevar (from heaptypeVar) 

syntax heaptype = 
   | AnyHT (from heaptype) 
   | NoneHT (from heaptype) 
   | EqHT (from heaptype) 
   | I31HT (from heaptype) 
   | StructHT (from heaptype) 
   | ArrayHT (from heaptype) 
   | FuncHT (from heaptype) 
   | NoFuncHT (from heaptype) 
   | ExnHT (from heaptype) 
   | NoExnHT (from heaptype) 
   | ExternHT (from heaptype) 
   | NoExternHT (from heaptype) 
   | VarHT typevar (from heaptypeVar) 
   | DefHT deftype (from heaptype) 
   | BotHT (from heaptype) 

syntax heaptypeConcrete = 
   | AnyHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | NoneHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | EqHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | I31HT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | StructHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | ArrayHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | FuncHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | NoFuncHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | ExnHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | NoExnHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | ExternHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | NoExternHT (from heaptypeConcrete) 
   | VarHT typevar (from heaptypeVar) 

syntax reftype = 
   | null heaptype (from reftype) 

syntax valtype = 
   | NumT numtype (from valtype) 
   | VecT vectype (from valtype) 
   | RefT reftype (from valtype) 
   | BotT (from valtype) 

syntax valtypeConcrete = 
   | NumT numtype (from valtypeConcrete) 
   | VecT vectype (from valtypeConcrete) 
   | RefT reftype (from valtypeConcrete)

   | InstrT resulttype resulttype localidx* (from instrtype)

   | I8 (from packtype)

   | ValStorageT valtype (from storagetype)

   | FieldT mut storagetype (from fieldtype) 

syntax structtype = 
   | StructT fieldtype* (from structtype) 

syntax arraytype = 
   | ArrayT fieldtype (from arraytype)

   | FuncT resulttype resulttype (from functype) 

syntax strtype = 
   | DefStructT structtype (from strtype)

   | SubT final heaptype* strtype (from subtype)

   | SubT final heaptypeVar* strtype (from subtypeConcrete)

   | RecT subtype* (from rectype)

   | DefT rectype n (from deftype)

syntax globaltype = 
   | GlobalT mut valtype (from globaltype) 

syntax tagtype = 
   | TagT deftype (from tagtype) 

syntax localtype = 
   | LocalT init valtype (from localtype) 

syntax elemtype = reftype

syntax datatype = 
   | OK (from datatype) 

syntax externtype = 
   | ExtFunct deftype (from externtype) 
   | ExtTableT tabletype (from externtype) 
   | ExtMemoryT memtype (from externtype) 
   | ExtGlobalT globaltype (from externtype) 
   | ExtTagT tagtype (from externtype)

   | ExportT externtype name (from exporttype) 

syntax importtype = 
   | ImportT externtype name name (from importtype)

   | ModuleT importtype* exporttype* (from moduletype)

def $subst_rectype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, rect)

1. (Let (RecT (st)*{st <- st*}) be rect)

  1. (Let st' be $subst_subtype<X>(X, $s, st))*{st <- st*, st' -> st'*}

    1. Return (RecT (st')*{st' <- st'*})

def $subst_subtype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, st)

1. (Let (SubT fin (ht)*{ht <- ht*} strt) be st)

  1. (Let ht' be $subst_heaptype<X>(X, $s, ht))*{ht <- ht*, ht' -> ht'*}

    1. (Let strt' be $subst_strtype<X>(X, $s, strt))

      1. Return (SubT fin (ht')*{ht' <- ht'*} strt')

def $subst_heaptype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, ht)

1. If ((((((((((((((ht = (AnyHT)) \/ (ht = (NoneHT))) \/ (ht = (EqHT))) \/ (ht = (I31HT))) \/ (ht = (StructHT))) \/ (ht = (ArrayHT))) \/ (ht = (FuncHT))) \/ (ht = (NoFuncHT))) \/ (ht = (ExnHT))) \/ (ht = (NoExnHT))) \/ (ht = (ExternHT))) \/ (ht = (NoExternHT))) \/ (ht = (BotHT)))), then

  1. Return ht

1. Else Dangling#87

2. Case analysis on ht

  1. Case (% has type heaptypeVar)

    1. (Let (VarHT tv) be (ht as heaptypeVar))

      1. Return $s(tv, X)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `DefHT %`)

    1. (Let (DefHT dt) be ht)

      1. Return (DefHT dt)

2. Else Dangling#89

def $subst_strtype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, strt)

1. Case analysis on strt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `DefStructT %`)

    1. (Let (DefStructT structtype) be strt)

      1. (Let structtype' be $subst_structtype<X>(X, $s, structtype))

        1. Return (DefStructT structtype')

  2. Case (% matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)

    1. (Let (DefArrayT arraytype) be strt)

      1. (Let arraytype' be $subst_arraytype<X>(X, $s, arraytype))

        1. Return (DefArrayT arraytype')

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)

    1. (Let (DefFuncT functype) be strt)

      1. (Let functype' be $subst_functype<X>(X, $s, functype))

        1. Return (DefFuncT functype')

def $subst_structtype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (StructT (yt)*{yt <- yt*}))

1. (Let yt' be $subst_fieldtype<X>(X, $s, yt))*{yt <- yt*, yt' -> yt'*}

  1. Return (StructT (yt')*{yt' <- yt'*})

def $subst_arraytype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (ArrayT yt))

1. (Let yt' be $subst_fieldtype<X>(X, $s, yt))

  1. Return (ArrayT yt')

def $subst_functype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (FuncT resulttype_1 resulttype_2))

1. (Let resulttype_1' be $subst_resulttype<X>(X, $s, resulttype_1))

  1. (Let resulttype_2' be $subst_resulttype<X>(X, $s, resulttype_2))

    1. Return (FuncT resulttype_1' resulttype_2')

def $subst_resulttype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (t)*{t <- t*})

1. (Let t' be $subst_valtype<X>(X, $s, t))*{t <- t*, t' -> t'*}

  1. Return (t')*{t' <- t'*}

def $subst_valtype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, t)

1. Case analysis on t

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NumT %`)

    1. (Let (NumT nt) be t)

      1. Return (NumT nt)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `VecT %`)

    1. (Let (VecT vt) be t)

      1. Return (VecT vt)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `RefT %`)

    1. (Let (RefT rt) be t)

      1. (Let rt' be $subst_reftype<X>(X, $s, rt))

        1. Return (RefT rt')

  4. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return (BotT)

def $subst_reftype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (null ht))

1. (Let ht' be $subst_heaptype<X>(X, $s, ht))

  1. Return (null ht')

def $subst_storagetype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, zt)

1. Case analysis on zt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ValStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (ValStorageT t) be zt)

      1. (Let t' be $subst_valtype<X>(X, $s, t))

        1. Return (ValStorageT t')

  2. Case (% matches pattern `PackStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (PackStorageT pt) be zt)

      1. Return (PackStorageT pt)

def $subst_fieldtype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (FieldT mut zt))

1. (Let zt' be $subst_storagetype<X>(X, $s, zt))

  1. Return (FieldT mut zt')

def $subst_deftype<X>(X, $s(tv, X) : heaptype, (DefT rect n))

1. (Let rect' be $subst_rectype<X>(X, $s, rect))

  1. Return (DefT rect' n)

def $s_roll(tv, (x, y))

1. If ((tv matches pattern `StatX %`)), then

  1. (Let (StatX x') be tv)

    1. If (((x <= x') /\ (x' < y))), then

      1. (Let int be (x' - x))

        1. If ((int has type idx)), then

          1. (Let x'' be (int as idx))

            1. Return ((VarHT (RecX x'')) as heaptype)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return ((VarHT tv) as heaptype)

def $s_unroll(tv, rect)

1. If ((tv matches pattern `RecX %`)), then

  1. (Let (RecX n) be tv)

    1. Return (DefHT (DefT rect n))

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return ((VarHT tv) as heaptype)

def $rollrt(x, rect)

1. (Let (RecT (st)*{st <- st*}) be rect)

  1. (Let y be (|(st)*{st <- st*}| + x))

    1. Return $subst_rectype<(nat, nat)>((x, y), $s_roll, rect)

def $unrollrt(rect)

1. Return $subst_rectype<rectype>(rect, $s_unroll, rect)

def $rolldt(x, rect)

1. (Let rect' be $rollrt(x, rect))

  1. Return $rolldt'(0, rect')

def $unrolldt(dt)

1. (Let (DefT rect n) be dt)

  1. (Let (RecT (st)*{st <- st*}) be $unrollrt(rect))

    1. If ((n < |(st)*{st <- st*}|)), then

      1. Return (st)*{st <- st*}[n]

    1. Else Dangling#154

def $expanddt(dt)

1. (Let (SubT _fin (_ht)*{_ht <- _ht*} strt) be $unrolldt(dt))

  1. Return strt

def $rolldt'(i, rect)

1. (Let (RecT (st)*{st <- st*}) be rect)

  1. If ((i < |(st)*{st <- st*}|)), then

    1. Return (DefT rect i) :: $rolldt'((i + 1), rect)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return []

def $numtype_of_addrtype(at)

1. Case analysis on at

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I32AT`)

    1. Return (I32T)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I64AT`)

    1. Return (I64T)

def $functype_of_strtype(strt)

1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)), then

  1. (Let (DefFuncT ft) be strt)

    1. Return ft

1. Else Dangling#165

def $min_addrtype(at_1, at_2)

1. If (((at_1 = (I64AT)) /\ (at_2 = (I64AT)))), then

  1. Return (I64AT)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return (I32AT)

def $num_size(nt)

1. If (((nt = (I32T)) \/ (nt = (F32T)))), then

  1. Return (4 as int)

1. Else Dangling#171

2. If (((nt = (I64T)) \/ (nt = (F64T)))), then

  1. Return (8 as int)

2. Else Dangling#173

def $vec_size(vt)

1. Return (16 as int)

def $pack_size(pt)

1. Case analysis on pt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I8`)

    1. Return (1 as int)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I16`)

    1. Return (2 as int)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `I32`)

    1. Return (4 as int)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `I64`)

    1. Return (8 as int)

def $unpacked_storage_type(zt)

1. Case analysis on zt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ValStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (ValStorageT t) be zt)

      1. Return t

  2. Case (% matches pattern `PackStorageT %`)

    1. Return (NumT (I32T))

def $unpacked_field_type((FieldT mut zt))

1. Return $unpacked_storage_type(zt)

def $is_num_type(valtype)

1. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NumT %`)

    1. Return true

  2. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $is_vec_type(valtype)

1. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `VecT %`)

    1. Return true

  2. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $is_packed_storage_type(zt)

1. Case analysis on zt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ValStorageT %`)

    1. Return false

  2. Case (% matches pattern `PackStorageT %`)

    1. Return true

def $diff_reftype((null_1 ht_1), (null ht_2))

1. Case analysis on null

  1. Case (% matches pattern `Null`)

    1. Return ((NoNull) ht_1)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `NoNull`)

    1. Return (null_1 ht_1)

def $defaultable(valtype)

1. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NumT %`)

    1. Return true

  2. Case (% matches pattern `VecT %`)

    1. Return true

  3. Case (% matches pattern `RefT %`)

    1. (Let (RefT (null ht)) be valtype)

      1. Return (null = (Null))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return false

syntax vecop<A> = 
   | V128 A (from vecop<A>) 

syntax laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F> = 
   | I8x16 A (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | I16x8 B (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | I32x4 C (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | I64x2 D (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | F32x4 E (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 
   | F64x2 F (from laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>) 

syntax op<A, B, C, D> = 
   | I32 A (from op<A, B, C, D>) 
   | I64 B (from op<A, B, C, D>) 
   | F32 C (from op<A, B, C, D>)

def $type_num<A, B, C, D>(op<A, B, C, D>)

1. Case analysis on op<A, B, C, D>

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I32 %`)

    1. Return (I32T)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I64 %`)

    1. Return (I64T)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `F32 %`)

    1. Return (F32T)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `F64 %`)

    1. Return (F64T)

def $type_vecop<A>((V128 _A))

1. Return (V128T)

def $type_cvtop(op<icvtop, icvtop, fcvtop, fcvtop>)

1. Case analysis on op<icvtop, icvtop, fcvtop, fcvtop>

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I32 %`)

    1. (Let (I32 icvtop) be op<icvtop, icvtop, fcvtop, fcvtop>)

      1. Case analysis on icvtop

        1. Case (% is in [(WrapI64)])

          1. Return ((I64T), (I32T))

        2. Case (% is in [(TruncSF32), (TruncUF32), (TruncSatSF32), (TruncSatUF32), (ReinterpretFloat)])

          1. Return ((F32T), (I32T))

        3. Case (% is in [(TruncSF64), (TruncUF64), (TruncSatSF64), (TruncSatUF64)])

          1. Return ((F64T), (I32T))

      1. Else Dangling#214

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I64 %`)

    1. (Let (I64 icvtop) be op<icvtop, icvtop, fcvtop, fcvtop>)

      1. Case analysis on icvtop

        1. Case (% is in [(ExtendSI32), (ExtendUI32)])

          1. Return ((I32T), (I64T))

        2. Case (% is in [(TruncSF32), (TruncUF32), (TruncSatSF32), (TruncSatUF32)])

          1. Return ((F32T), (I64T))

        3. Case (% is in [(TruncSF64), (TruncUF64), (TruncSatSF64), (TruncSatUF64), (ReinterpretFloat)])

          1. Return ((F64T), (I64T))

      1. Else Dangling#219

  3. Case (% matches pattern `F32 %`)

    1. (Let (F32 fcvtop) be op<icvtop, icvtop, fcvtop, fcvtop>)

      1. Case analysis on fcvtop

        1. Case (% is in [(ConvertSI32), (ConvertUI32), (ReinterpretInt)])

          1. Return ((I32T), (F32T))

        2. Case (% is in [(ConvertSI64), (ConvertUI64)])

          1. Return ((I64T), (F32T))

        3. Case (% is in [(DemoteF64)])

          1. Return ((F64T), (F32T))

      1. Else Dangling#224

  4. Case (% matches pattern `F64 %`)

    1. (Let (F64 fcvtop) be op<icvtop, icvtop, fcvtop, fcvtop>)

      1. Case analysis on fcvtop

        1. Case (% is in [(ConvertSI32), (ConvertUI32)])

          1. Return ((I32T), (F64T))

        2. Case (% is in [(ConvertSI64), (ConvertUI64), (ReinterpretInt)])

          1. Return ((I64T), (F64T))

        3. Case (% is in [(PromoteF32)])

          1. Return ((F32T), (F64T))

      1. Else Dangling#229

def $type_lane<A, B, C, D, E, F>(laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>)

1. Case analysis on laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I8x16 %`)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I16x8 %`)

    1. Return (I32T)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `I32x4 %`)

    1. Return (I32T)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `I64x2 %`)

    1. Return (I64T)

  5. Case (% matches pattern `F32x4 %`)

    1. Return (F32T)

  6. Case (% matches pattern `F64x2 %`)

    1. Return (F64T)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `I64x2 %`)

    1. (Let (I64x2 (Replace i)) be laneop<vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop>)

      1. Return (i as int)

  5. Case (% matches pattern `F32x4 %`)

    1. (Let (F32x4 (Replace i)) be laneop<vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop>)

      1. Return (i as int)

  6. Case (% matches pattern `F64x2 %`)

    1. (Let (F64x2 (Replace i)) be laneop<vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop, vnreplaceop>)

      1. Return (i as int)

def $num_lanes<A, B, C, D, E, F>((V128 laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>))

1. Case analysis on laneop<A, B, C, D, E, F>

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I8x16 %`)

    1. Return (16 as int)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I16x8 %`)

    1. Return (8 as int)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `I32x4 %`)

    1. Return (4 as int)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `I64x2 %`)

    1. Return (2 as int)

  5. Case (% matches pattern `F32x4 %`)

    1. Return (4 as int)

  6. Case (% matches pattern `F64x2 %`)

    1. Return (2 as int)

def $type_externop(eop)

1. Case analysis on eop

  1. Case (% matches pattern `Internalize`)

    1. Return ((ExternHT), (AnyHT))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `Externalize`)

    1. Return ((AnyHT), (ExternHT))

syntax type = rectype

syntax global = {GTYPE globaltype, GINIT const}

syntax local = {LTYPE valtype}

syntax table = {TTYPE tabletype, TINIT const}

syntax memory = {MTYPE memtype}

syntax tag = {TGTYPE typeidx}

syntax func = {FTYPE typeidx, LOCALS local*, BODY const}

syntax start = {SFUNC funcidx}

syntax active = {INDEX idx, OFFSET const}

syntax elemmode = 
   | Passive (from elemmode) 
   | Active active (from elemmode) 
   | Declarative (from elemmode) 

syntax elem = {ETYPE reftype, EINIT const*, EMODE elemmode}

syntax datamode = 
   | Passive (from datamode) 
   | Active active (from datamode) 

syntax data = {DINIT text, DMODE datamode}

syntax importdesc = 
   | FuncImport typeidx (from importdesc) 
   | TableImport tabletype (from importdesc) 
   | MemImport memtype (from importdesc) 
   | GlobalImport globaltype (from importdesc) 
   | TagImport typeidx (from importdesc) 

syntax exportdesc = 
   | FuncExport funcidx (from exportdesc) 
   | TableExport tableidx (from exportdesc) 
   | MemExport memidx (from exportdesc) 
   | GlobalExport globalidx (from exportdesc) 
   | TagExport tagidx (from exportdesc) 

syntax import = {MODULENAME name, ITEMNAME name, IDESC importdesc}

syntax export = {NAME name, EDESC exportdesc}

syntax module = {TYPES type*, GLOBALS global*, TABLES table*, MEMS memory*, TAGS tag*, FUNCS func*, START start?, ELEMS elem*, DATAS data*, IMPORTS import*, EXPORTS export*}

var ty : type

var loc : local

var glob : global

var tab : table

var im : import

var ex : export

syntax free = {TYPES set<typeidx>, GLOBALS set<globalidx>, TABLES set<tableidx>, MEMS set<memidx>, TAGS set<tagidx>, FUNCS set<funcidx>, ELEMS set<elemidx>, DATAS set<dataidx>, LOCALS set<localidx>, LABELS set<labelidx>}

def $free_empty

1. Return {TYPES $empty_set<typeidx>, GLOBALS $empty_set<globalidx>, TABLES $empty_set<tableidx>, MEMS $empty_set<memidx>, TAGS $empty_set<tagidx>, FUNCS $empty_set<funcidx>, ELEMS $empty_set<elemidx>, DATAS $empty_set<dataidx>, LOCALS $empty_set<localidx>, LABELS $empty_set<labelidx>}

def $fold_left<X, Y>($f(X, Y) : X, X, (Y)*{Y <- Y*})

1. Case analysis on (Y)*{Y <- Y*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return X

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let Y_h :: (Y_t)*{Y_t <- Y_t*} be (Y)*{Y <- Y*})

      1. Return $fold_left<X, Y>($f, $f(X, Y_h), (Y_t)*{Y_t <- Y_t*})

def $free_union(free_1, free_2)

1. Return {TYPES $union_set<typeidx>(free_1.TYPES, free_2.TYPES), GLOBALS $union_set<globalidx>(free_1.GLOBALS, free_2.GLOBALS), TABLES $union_set<tableidx>(free_1.TABLES, free_2.TABLES), MEMS $union_set<memidx>(free_1.MEMS, free_2.MEMS), TAGS $union_set<tagidx>(free_1.TAGS, free_2.TAGS), FUNCS $union_set<funcidx>(free_1.FUNCS, free_2.FUNCS), ELEMS $union_set<elemidx>(free_1.ELEMS, free_2.ELEMS), DATAS $union_set<dataidx>(free_1.DATAS, free_2.DATAS), LOCALS $union_set<localidx>(free_1.LOCALS, free_2.LOCALS), LABELS $union_set<labelidx>(free_1.LABELS, free_2.LABELS)}

def $free_unions((free'')*{free'' <- free''*})

1. Case analysis on (free'')*{free'' <- free''*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return $free_empty

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let free_h :: (free_t)*{free_t <- free_t*} be (free'')*{free'' <- free''*})

      1. (Let free' be $free_unions((free_t)*{free_t <- free_t*}))

        1. (Let free be $free_union(free_h, free'))

          1. Return free

def $add_minus_one(x)

1. (Let int be (x - 1))

  1. If ((int has type idx)), then

    1. (Let x' be (int as idx))

      1. Return x'

  1. Else Dangling#302

def $free_idx(x)

1. Return (`{ [x] })

def $free_shift((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. (Let (`{ (x')*{x' <- x'*} }) be $diff_set<idx>((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }), (`{ [0] })))

  1. (Let (`{ (x'')*{x'' <- x''*} }) be $map_set<idx, idx>($add_minus_one, (`{ (x')*{x' <- x'*} })))

    1. Return (`{ (x'')*{x'' <- x''*} })

def $free_list<X>($free_(X) : free, (X)*{X <- X*})

1. Case analysis on (X)*{X <- X*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return $free_empty

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let X_h :: (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*} be (X)*{X <- X*})

      1. Return $fold_left<free, free>($free_union, $free_(X_h), [$free_list<X>($free_, (X_t)*{X_t <- X_t*})])

def $free_opt<X>($free_(X) : free, (X')?{X' <- X'?})

1. Case analysis on (X')?{X' <- X'?}

  1. Case (% matches pattern ())

    1. Return $free_empty

  2. Case (% matches pattern (_))

    1. (Let ?(X) be (X')?{X' <- X'?})

      1. Return $free_(X)

def $free_catch(catch)

1. Case analysis on catch

  1. Case (% matches pattern `Catch % %`)

    1. (Let (Catch x y) be catch)

      1. Return $free_union($update_tags($free_idx(x)), $update_labels($free_idx(y)))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `CatchRef % %`)

    1. (Let (CatchRef x y) be catch)

      1. Return $free_union($update_tags($free_idx(x)), $update_labels($free_idx(y)))

  3. Case (% matches pattern `CatchAll %`)

    1. (Let (CatchAll x) be catch)

      1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `CatchAllRef %`)

    1. (Let (CatchAllRef x) be catch)

      1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

def $free_type_(type)

1. Return $free_rectype(type)

def $free_global(global)

1. (Let free_1 be $free_globaltype(global.GTYPE))

  1. (Let free_2 be $free_const(global.GINIT))

    1. (Let free be $free_union(free_1, free_2))

      1. Return free

def $free_table(table)

1. (Let free_1 be $free_tabletype(table.TTYPE))

  1. (Let free_2 be $free_const(table.TINIT))

    1. (Let free be $free_union(free_1, free_2))

      1. Return free

def $free_mem({MTYPE mt})

1. Return $free_memtype(mt)

def $free_func(func)

1. (Let free_1 be $update_types((`{ [func.FTYPE] })))

  1. (Let free_2 be $free_block(func.BODY))

    1. (Let free be $free_union(free_1, free_2))

      1. Return free[LOCALS = $empty_set<localidx>]

def $free_start(start)

1. Return $update_funcs((`{ [start.SFUNC] }))

def $free_elem(elem)

1. (Let free_1 be $free_list<const>($free_const, elem.EINIT))

  1. (Let free_2 be $free_elemmode($update_tables, elem.EMODE))

    1. (Let free be $free_union(free_1, free_2))

      1. Return free

def $free_data(data)

1. Return $free_datamode($update_mems, data.DMODE)

def $free_import(import)

1. Return $free_importdesc(import.IDESC)

def $free_export(export)

1. Return $free_exportdesc(export.EDESC)

def $free_rectype((RecT (st)*{st <- st*}))

1. Return $free_list<subtype>($free_subtype, (st)*{st <- st*})

def $free_subtype((SubT fin (ht)*{ht <- ht*} strt))

1. Return $free_union($free_list<heaptype>($free_heaptype, (ht)*{ht <- ht*}), $free_strtype(strt))

def $free_heaptype(ht)

1. If ((ht has type heaptypeVar)), then

  1. (Let (VarHT tv) be (ht as heaptypeVar))

    1. Return $free_typevar(tv)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return $free_empty

def $free_typevar(tv)

1. Case analysis on tv

  1. Case (% matches pattern `StatX %`)

    1. (Let (StatX x) be tv)

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `RecX %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

def $free_strtype(strt)

1. Case analysis on strt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `DefStructT %`)

    1. (Let (DefStructT strctt) be strt)

      1. Return $free_structtype(strctt)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)

    1. (Let (DefArrayT arrt) be strt)

      1. Return $free_arraytype(arrt)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)

    1. (Let (DefFuncT ft) be strt)

      1. Return $free_functype(ft)

def $free_structtype((StructT (yt)*{yt <- yt*}))

1. Return $free_list<fieldtype>($free_fieldtype, (yt)*{yt <- yt*})

def $free_arraytype((ArrayT yt))

1. Return $free_fieldtype(yt)

def $free_functype((FuncT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}))

1. Return $free_union($free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}), $free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}))

def $free_fieldtype((FieldT _mut zt))

1. Return $free_storagetype(zt)

def $free_storagetype(zt)

1. Case analysis on zt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ValStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (ValStorageT t) be zt)

      1. Return $free_valtype(t)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `PackStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (PackStorageT pt) be zt)

      1. Return $free_packtype(pt)

def $free_valtype(valtype)

1. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NumT %`)

    1. (Let (NumT nt) be valtype)

      1. Return $free_numtype(nt)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `VecT %`)

    1. (Let (VecT vt) be valtype)

      1. Return $free_vectype(vt)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `RefT %`)

    1. (Let (RefT rt) be valtype)

      1. Return $free_reftype(rt)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return $free_empty

def $free_packtype(pt)

1. Return $free_empty

def $free_numtype(nt)

1. Return $free_empty

def $free_vectype(vt)

1. Return $free_empty

def $free_reftype((_null ht))

1. Return $free_heaptype(ht)

def $free_globaltype((GlobalT _mut t))

1. Return $free_valtype(t)

def $free_blocktype(blocktype)

1. Case analysis on blocktype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `VarBlockType %`)

    1. (Let (VarBlockType x) be blocktype)

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `ValBlockType %`)

    1. (Let (ValBlockType (valtype)?{valtype <- valtype?}) be blocktype)

      1. If (((valtype)?{valtype <- valtype?} matches pattern (_))), then

        1. (Let ?(t) be (valtype)?{valtype <- valtype?})

          1. Return $free_valtype(t)

      1. Else Dangling#391

2. If ((blocktype = (ValBlockType ?()))), then

  1. Return $free_empty

2. Else Dangling#394

def $free_instr(instr')

1. Case analysis on instr'

  1. Case (% matches pattern `UNREACHABLE`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  2. Case (% matches pattern `NOP`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DROP`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  4. Case (% matches pattern `SELECT %`)

    1. (Let (SELECT ((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})?{valtype* <- valtype*?}) be instr')

      1. If ((((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})?{valtype* <- valtype*?} matches pattern (_))), then

        1. (Let ?((t)*{t <- t*}) be ((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})?{valtype* <- valtype*?})

          1. Return $free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, (t)*{t <- t*})

  5. Case (% matches pattern `BLOCK % %`)

    1. (Let (BLOCK blocktype (instr)*{instr <- instr*}) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_block((instr)*{instr <- instr*}))

  6. Case (% matches pattern `LOOP % %`)

    1. (Let (LOOP blocktype (instr)*{instr <- instr*}) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_block((instr)*{instr <- instr*}))

  7. Case (% matches pattern `IF % % ELSE %`)

    1. (Let (IF blocktype (instr_t)*{instr_t <- instr_t*} ELSE (instr_f)*{instr_f <- instr_f*}) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_union($free_block((instr_t)*{instr_t <- instr_t*}), $free_block((instr_f)*{instr_f <- instr_f*})))

  8. Case (% matches pattern `BR %`)

    1. (Let (BR x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  9. Case (% matches pattern `BR_IF %`)

    1. (Let (BR_IF x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  10. Case (% matches pattern `BR_TABLE % %`)

    1. (Let (BR_TABLE (x)*{x <- x*} y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_list<idx>($update_label, y :: (x)*{x <- x*})

  11. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_NULL %`)

    1. (Let (BR_ON_NULL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  12. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_NON_NULL %`)

    1. (Let (BR_ON_NON_NULL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

  13. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_CAST % % %`)

    1. (Let (BR_ON_CAST x rt_1 rt_2) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_labels($free_idx(x)), $free_union($free_reftype(rt_1), $free_reftype(rt_2)))

  14. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_CAST_FAIL % % %`)

    1. (Let (BR_ON_CAST_FAIL x rt_1 rt_2) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_labels($free_idx(x)), $free_union($free_reftype(rt_1), $free_reftype(rt_2)))

  15. Case (% matches pattern `CALL %`)

    1. (Let (CALL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  16. Case (% matches pattern `CALL_REF %`)

    1. (Let (CALL_REF x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  17. Case (% matches pattern `CALL_INDIRECT % %`)

    1. (Let (CALL_INDIRECT x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_types($free_idx(y)))

  18. Case (% matches pattern `RETURN`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  19. Case (% matches pattern `RETURN_CALL %`)

    1. (Let (RETURN_CALL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  20. Case (% matches pattern `RETURN_CALL_REF %`)

    1. (Let (RETURN_CALL_REF x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  21. Case (% matches pattern `RETURN_CALL_INDIRECT % %`)

    1. (Let (RETURN_CALL_INDIRECT x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_types($free_idx(y)))

  22. Case (% matches pattern `THROW %`)

    1. (Let (THROW x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_tags($free_idx(x))

  23. Case (% matches pattern `THROW_REF`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  24. Case (% matches pattern `TRY_TABLE % % %`)

    1. (Let (TRY_TABLE blocktype (catch)*{catch <- catch*} (instr)*{instr <- instr*}) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($free_blocktype(blocktype), $free_union($free_list<catch>($free_catch, (catch)*{catch <- catch*}), $free_block((instr)*{instr <- instr*})))

  25. Case (% matches pattern `LOCAL.GET %`)

    1. (Let (LOCAL.GET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_locals($free_idx(x))

  26. Case (% matches pattern `LOCAL.SET %`)

    1. (Let (LOCAL.SET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_locals($free_idx(x))

  27. Case (% matches pattern `LOCAL.TEE %`)

    1. (Let (LOCAL.TEE x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_locals($free_idx(x))

  28. Case (% matches pattern `GLOBAL.GET %`)

    1. (Let (GLOBAL.GET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_globals($free_idx(x))

  29. Case (% matches pattern `GLOBAL.SET %`)

    1. (Let (GLOBAL.SET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_globals($free_idx(x))

  30. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.GET %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.GET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  31. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.SET %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.SET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  32. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.SIZE %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.SIZE x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  33. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.GROW %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.GROW x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  34. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.FILL %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.FILL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  35. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.COPY % %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.COPY x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_tables($free_idx(y)))

  36. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.INIT % %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.INIT x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_tables($free_idx(x)), $update_elems($free_idx(y)))

  37. Case (% matches pattern `ELEM.DROP %`)

    1. (Let (ELEM.DROP x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_elems($free_idx(x))

  38. Case (% matches pattern `LOAD % %`)

    1. (Let (LOAD x _lop) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  39. Case (% matches pattern `STORE % %`)

    1. (Let (STORE x _sop) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  40. Case (% matches pattern `VEC.LOAD % %`)

    1. (Let (VEC.LOAD x _vlop) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  41. Case (% matches pattern `VEC.STORE % %`)

    1. (Let (VEC.STORE x _vsop) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  42. Case (% matches pattern `VEC.LOAD_LANE % % %`)

    1. (Let (VEC.LOAD_LANE x _vlaneop _int) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  43. Case (% matches pattern `VEC.STORE_LANE % % %`)

    1. (Let (VEC.STORE_LANE x _vlaneop _int) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  44. Case (% matches pattern `MEMORY.SIZE %`)

    1. (Let (MEMORY.SIZE x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  45. Case (% matches pattern `MEMORY.GROW %`)

    1. (Let (MEMORY.GROW x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  46. Case (% matches pattern `MEMORY.FILL %`)

    1. (Let (MEMORY.FILL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  47. Case (% matches pattern `MEMORY.COPY % %`)

    1. (Let (MEMORY.COPY x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_mems($free_idx(x)), $update_mems($free_idx(y)))

  48. Case (% matches pattern `MEMORY.INIT % %`)

    1. (Let (MEMORY.INIT x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_mems($free_idx(x)), $update_datas($free_idx(y)))

  49. Case (% matches pattern `DATA.DROP %`)

    1. (Let (DATA.DROP x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_datas($free_idx(x))

  50. Case (% matches pattern `REF.NULL %`)

    1. (Let (REF.NULL ht) be instr')

      1. Return $free_heaptype(ht)

  51. Case (% matches pattern `REF.FUNC %`)

    1. (Let (REF.FUNC x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  52. Case (% matches pattern `REF.IS_NULL`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  53. Case (% matches pattern `REF.AS_NON_NULL`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  54. Case (% matches pattern `REF.TEST %`)

    1. (Let (REF.TEST rt) be instr')

      1. Return $free_reftype(rt)

  55. Case (% matches pattern `REF.CAST %`)

    1. (Let (REF.CAST rt) be instr')

      1. Return $free_reftype(rt)

  56. Case (% matches pattern `REF.EQ`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  57. Case (% matches pattern `REF.I31`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  58. Case (% matches pattern `I31.GET %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  59. Case (% matches pattern `STRUCT.NEW % %`)

    1. (Let (STRUCT.NEW x _iop) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  60. Case (% matches pattern `STRUCT.GET % % %`)

    1. (Let (STRUCT.GET x _ti (_extension)?{_extension <- _extension?}) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  61. Case (% matches pattern `STRUCT.SET % %`)

    1. (Let (STRUCT.SET x _ti) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  62. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.NEW x _iop) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  63. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW_FIXED % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.NEW_FIXED x _u32) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  64. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW_ELEM % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.NEW_ELEM x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_elems($free_idx(y)))

  65. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW_DATA % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.NEW_DATA x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_datas($free_idx(y)))

  66. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.GET % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.GET x (_extension)?{_extension <- _extension?}) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  67. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.SET %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.SET x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  68. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.LEN`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  69. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.COPY % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.COPY x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_types($free_idx(y)))

  70. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.FILL %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.FILL x) be instr')

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  71. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.INIT_DATA % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.INIT_DATA x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_datas($free_idx(y)))

  72. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.INIT_ELEM % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.INIT_ELEM x y) be instr')

      1. Return $free_union($update_types($free_idx(x)), $update_elems($free_idx(y)))

  73. Case (% matches pattern `EXTERN.CONVERT %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  74. Case (% matches pattern `CONST %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  75. Case (% matches pattern `TEST %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  76. Case (% matches pattern `COMPARE %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  77. Case (% matches pattern `UNARY %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  78. Case (% matches pattern `BINOP %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  79. Case (% matches pattern `CONVERT %`)

    1. Return $free_empty

2. If ((instr' = (SELECT ?()))), then

  1. Return $free_list<valtype>($free_valtype, [])

3. Otherwise,

  1. Return $free_empty

def $free_block((instr)*{instr <- instr*})

1. (Let free be $free_list<instr>($free_instr, (instr)*{instr <- instr*}))

  1. (Let free' be free[LABELS = $free_shift(free.LABELS)])

    1. Return free'

def $free_const((instr)*{instr <- instr*})

1. Return $free_block((instr)*{instr <- instr*})

def $update_types((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[TYPES = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_globals((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[GLOBALS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_tables((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[TABLES = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_mems((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[MEMS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_tags((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[TAGS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_funcs((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[FUNCS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_elems((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[ELEMS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_datas((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[DATAS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_locals((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[LOCALS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_labels((`{ (x)*{x <- x*} }))

1. Return $free_empty[LABELS = (`{ (x)*{x <- x*} })]

def $update_label(x)

1. Return $update_labels($free_idx(x))

def $free_tabletype((TableT _at _lim rt))

1. Return $free_reftype(rt)

def $free_memtype((MemoryT _at _lim))

1. Return $free_empty

def $free_elemmode($f(set<tableidx>) : free, elemmode)

1. If (((elemmode = (Passive)) \/ (elemmode = (Declarative)))), then

  1. Return $free_empty

1. Else Dangling#559

2. If ((elemmode matches pattern `Active %`)), then

  1. (Let (Active {INDEX idx, OFFSET (instr)*{instr <- instr*}}) be elemmode)

    1. (Let free_1 be $f((`{ [idx] })))

      1. (Let free_2 be $free_const((instr)*{instr <- instr*}))

        1. Return $free_union(free_1, free_2)

2. Else Dangling#561

def $free_datamode($f(set<memidx>) : free, datamode)

1. Case analysis on datamode

  1. Case (% matches pattern `Passive`)

    1. Return $free_empty

  2. Case (% matches pattern `Active %`)

    1. (Let (Active {INDEX idx, OFFSET (instr)*{instr <- instr*}}) be datamode)

      1. (Let free_1 be $f((`{ [idx] })))

        1. (Let free_2 be $free_const((instr)*{instr <- instr*}))

          1. Return $free_union(free_1, free_2)

def $free_importdesc(importdesc)

1. Case analysis on importdesc

  1. Case (% matches pattern `FuncImport %`)

    1. (Let (FuncImport x) be importdesc)

      1. Return $update_types($free_idx(x))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `TableImport %`)

    1. (Let (TableImport tt) be importdesc)

      1. Return $free_tabletype(tt)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `MemImport %`)

    1. (Let (MemImport mt) be importdesc)

      1. Return $free_memtype(mt)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `GlobalImport %`)

    1. (Let (GlobalImport gt) be importdesc)

      1. Return $free_globaltype(gt)

  5. Case (% matches pattern `TagImport %`)

    1. (Let (TagImport x) be importdesc)

      1. Return $update_tags($free_idx(x))

def $free_exportdesc(exportdesc)

1. Case analysis on exportdesc

  1. Case (% matches pattern `FuncExport %`)

    1. (Let (FuncExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. Return $update_funcs($free_idx(x))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `TableExport %`)

    1. (Let (TableExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. Return $update_tables($free_idx(x))

  3. Case (% matches pattern `MemExport %`)

    1. (Let (MemExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. Return $update_mems($free_idx(x))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `GlobalExport %`)

    1. (Let (GlobalExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. Return $update_globals($free_idx(x))

  5. Case (% matches pattern `TagExport %`)

    1. (Let (TagExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. Return $update_tags($free_idx(x))

def $free_module(module)

1. (Let free_1 be $free_list<type>($free_type_, module.TYPES))

  1. (Let free_2 be $free_list<global>($free_global, module.GLOBALS))

    1. (Let free_3 be $free_list<table>($free_table, module.TABLES))

      1. (Let free_4 be $free_list<memory>($free_mem, module.MEMS))

        1. (Let free_5 be $free_opt<start>($free_start, module.START))

          1. (Let free_6 be $free_list<elem>($free_elem, module.ELEMS))

            1. (Let free_7 be $free_list<data>($free_data, module.DATAS))

              1. (Let free_8 be $free_list<import>($free_import, module.IMPORTS))

                1. (Let free_9 be $free_list<export>($free_export, module.EXPORTS))

                  1. (Let free be $free_unions([free_1, free_2, free_3, free_4, free_5, free_6, free_7, free_8, free_9]))

                    1. Return free

def $match_null(null, null')

1. If ((null matches pattern `NoNull`)), then

  1. If ((null' matches pattern `Null`)), then

    1. Return true

2. If ((null = null')), then

  1. Return true

3. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $subst_of(tv, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Case analysis on tv

  1. Case (% matches pattern `StatX %`)

    1. (Let (StatX x) be tv)

      1. If ((x < |(dt)*{dt <- dt*}|)), then

        1. Return (DefHT (dt)*{dt <- dt*}[x])

      1. Else Dangling#613

  2. Case (% matches pattern `RecX %`)

    1. (Let (RecX x) be tv)

      1. Return ((VarHT (RecX x)) as heaptype)

def $match_deftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2)

1. If ((dt_1 = dt_2)), then

  1. Return true

1. Else Dangling#617

2. If (($subst_deftype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_1) = $subst_deftype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_2))), then

  1. Return true

2. Else Dangling#619

3. (Let (SubT fin (ht)*{ht <- ht*} strt) be $unrolldt(dt_1))

  1. Return $match_heaptype_exists((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (ht)*{ht <- ht*}, dt_2)

def $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht, ht')

1. Case analysis on ht'

  1. Case (% matches pattern `AnyHT`)

    1. If (((((ht = (EqHT)) \/ (ht = (StructHT))) \/ (ht = (ArrayHT))) \/ (ht = (I31HT)))), then

      1. Return true

  2. Case (% matches pattern `EqHT`)

    1. If ((((ht = (I31HT)) \/ (ht = (StructHT))) \/ (ht = (ArrayHT)))), then

      1. Return true

  3. Case (% has type heaptypeVar)

    1. (Let (VarHT tv) be (ht' as heaptypeVar))

      1. If ((tv matches pattern `StatX %`)), then

        1. (Let (StatX x) be tv)

          1. If ((x < |(dt)*{dt <- dt*}|)), then

            1. Return $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht, (DefHT (dt)*{dt <- dt*}[x]))

2. Case analysis on ht

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NoneHT`)

    1. Return $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht', (AnyHT))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `NoFuncHT`)

    1. Return $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht', (FuncHT))

  3. Case (% matches pattern `NoExnHT`)

    1. Return $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht', (ExnHT))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `NoExternHT`)

    1. Return $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht', (ExternHT))

  5. Case (% has type heaptypeVar)

    1. (Let (VarHT tv) be (ht as heaptypeVar))

      1. If ((tv matches pattern `StatX %`)), then

        1. (Let (StatX x) be tv)

          1. If ((x < |(dt)*{dt <- dt*}|)), then

            1. Return $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (DefHT (dt)*{dt <- dt*}[x]), ht')

  6. Case (% matches pattern `DefHT %`)

    1. (Let (DefHT dt_1) be ht)

      1. If ((ht' matches pattern `DefHT %`)), then

        1. (Let (DefHT dt_2) be ht')

          1. Return $match_deftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2)

      2. (Let strt be $expanddt(dt_1))

        1. Case analysis on strt

          1. Case (% matches pattern `DefStructT %`)

            1. If ((((ht' = (AnyHT)) \/ (ht' = (EqHT))) \/ (ht' = (StructHT)))), then

              1. Return true

          2. Case (% matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)

            1. If ((((ht' = (AnyHT)) \/ (ht' = (EqHT))) \/ (ht' = (ArrayHT)))), then

              1. Return true

      3. If ((ht' matches pattern `FuncHT`)), then

        1. (Let strt be $expanddt(dt_1))

          1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)), then

            1. Return true

  7. Case (% matches pattern `BotHT`)

    1. Return true

3. If ((ht = ht')), then

  1. Return true

4. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_heaptype_exists((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (ht)*{ht <- ht*}, dt_1)

1. Case analysis on (ht)*{ht <- ht*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return false

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let ht_h :: (ht_t)*{ht_t <- ht_t*} be (ht)*{ht <- ht*})

      1. Case analysis on $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht_h, (DefHT dt_1))

        1. Case true

          1. Return true

        2. Case false

          1. Return $match_heaptype_exists((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (ht_t)*{ht_t <- ht_t*}, dt_1)

def $match_numtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, nt_1, nt_2)

1. If ((nt_1 = nt_2)), then

  1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_vectype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, vt_1, vt_2)

1. Return (vt_1 = vt_2)

def $match_reftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, rt, rt')

1. (Let (null_1 ht_1) be rt)

  1. (Let (null_2 ht_2) be rt')

    1. If (($match_null(null_1, null_2) /\ $match_heaptype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht_1, ht_2))), then

      1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_valtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, valtype, valtype')

1. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NumT %`)

    1. (Let (NumT nt_1) be valtype)

      1. If ((valtype' matches pattern `NumT %`)), then

        1. (Let (NumT nt_2) be valtype')

          1. Return $match_numtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, nt_1, nt_2)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `VecT %`)

    1. (Let (VecT vt_1) be valtype)

      1. If ((valtype' matches pattern `VecT %`)), then

        1. (Let (VecT vt_2) be valtype')

          1. Return $match_vectype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, vt_1, vt_2)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `RefT %`)

    1. (Let (RefT rt_1) be valtype)

      1. If ((valtype' matches pattern `RefT %`)), then

        1. (Let (RefT rt_2) be valtype')

          1. Return $match_reftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, rt_1, rt_2)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_packtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, pt_1, pt_2)

1. Return (pt_1 = pt_2)

def $match_storagetype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, zt, zt')

1. Case analysis on zt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ValStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (ValStorageT t_1) be zt)

      1. If ((zt' matches pattern `ValStorageT %`)), then

        1. (Let (ValStorageT t_2) be zt')

          1. Return $match_valtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `PackStorageT %`)

    1. (Let (PackStorageT pt_1) be zt)

      1. If ((zt' matches pattern `PackStorageT %`)), then

        1. (Let (PackStorageT pt_2) be zt')

          1. Return $match_packtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, pt_1, pt_2)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_arraytype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (ArrayT yt_1), (ArrayT yt_2))

1. Return $match_fieldtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, yt_1, yt_2)

def $match_functype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (FuncT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}), (FuncT (t_1')*{t_1' <- t_1'*} (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*}))

1. Return ($match_resulttype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (t_1')*{t_1' <- t_1'*}, (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}) /\ $match_resulttype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}, (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*}))

def $match_fieldtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, yt, yt')

1. (Let (FieldT mut_1 zt_1) be yt)

  1. (Let (FieldT mut_2 zt_2) be yt')

    1. If ((((mut_1 = mut_2) /\ $match_storagetype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, zt_1, zt_2)) /\ (mut_1 = (Cons)))), then

      1. Return true

    2. If (((((mut_1 = mut_2) /\ $match_storagetype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, zt_1, zt_2)) /\ (mut_1 = (Var))) /\ $match_storagetype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, zt_2, zt_1))), then

      1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_structtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, strctt, strctt')

1. (Let (StructT (yt_1)*{yt_1 <- yt_1*}) be strctt)

  1. (Let (StructT (yt_2)*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}) be strctt')

    1. If ((|(yt_1)*{yt_1 <- yt_1*}| >= |(yt_2)*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}|)), then

      1. (Let (yt_1')*{yt_1' <- yt_1'*} be (yt_1)*{yt_1 <- yt_1*}[0 : |(yt_2)*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}|])

        1. If ((|(yt_1')*{yt_1' <- yt_1'*}| = |(yt_2)*{yt_2 <- yt_2*}|)), then

          1. If ($match_fieldtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, yt_1', yt_2))*{yt_1' <- yt_1'*, yt_2 <- yt_2*}, then

            1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_strtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, strt, strt')

1. Case analysis on strt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `DefStructT %`)

    1. (Let (DefStructT strctt_1) be strt)

      1. If ((strt' matches pattern `DefStructT %`)), then

        1. (Let (DefStructT strctt_2) be strt')

          1. Return $match_structtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, strctt_1, strctt_2)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)

    1. (Let (DefArrayT arrt_1) be strt)

      1. If ((strt' matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)), then

        1. (Let (DefArrayT arrt_2) be strt')

          1. Return $match_arraytype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, arrt_1, arrt_2)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)

    1. (Let (DefFuncT ft_1) be strt)

      1. If ((strt' matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)), then

        1. (Let (DefFuncT ft_2) be strt')

          1. Return $match_functype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ft_1, ft_2)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_resulttype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, rest, rest')

1. (Let (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} be rest)

  1. (Let (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} be rest')

    1. If ((|(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}| = |(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)), then

      1. If ($match_valtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2))*{t_1 <- t_1*, t_2 <- t_2*}, then

        1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $abs_of_str_type(strt)

1. Case analysis on strt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `DefStructT %`)

    1. Return (StructHT)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)

    1. Return (ArrayHT)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)

    1. Return (FuncHT)

def $top_of_str_type((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, strt)

1. Return $top_of_heap_type((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $abs_of_str_type(strt))

def $top_of_heap_type((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, ht)

1. Case analysis on ht

  1. Case (% is in [(AnyHT), (NoneHT), (EqHT), (StructHT), (ArrayHT), (I31HT)])

    1. Return (AnyHT)

  2. Case (% is in [(FuncHT), (NoFuncHT)])

    1. Return (FuncHT)

  3. Case (% is in [(ExnHT), (NoExnHT)])

    1. Return (ExnHT)

  4. Case (% is in [(ExternHT), (NoExternHT)])

    1. Return (ExternHT)

1. Else Dangling#744

2. Case analysis on ht

  1. Case (% matches pattern `DefHT %`)

    1. (Let (DefHT dt') be ht)

      1. Return $top_of_str_type((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $expanddt(dt'))

  2. Case (% has type heaptypeVar)

    1. (Let (VarHT tv) be (ht as heaptypeVar))

      1. If ((tv matches pattern `StatX %`)), then

        1. (Let (StatX x) be tv)

          1. If ((x < |(dt)*{dt <- dt*}|)), then

            1. Return $top_of_str_type((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $expanddt((dt)*{dt <- dt*}[x]))

          1. Else Dangling#755

      1. Else Dangling#753

2. Else Dangling#749

def $bot_of_str_type(strt)

1. Case analysis on strt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `DefStructT %`)

    1. Return (NoneHT)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)

    1. Return (NoneHT)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)

    1. Return (NoFuncHT)

def $bot_of_heap_type(ht)

1. Case analysis on ht

  1. Case (% is in [(AnyHT), (NoneHT), (EqHT), (StructHT), (ArrayHT), (I31HT)])

    1. Return (NoneHT)

  2. Case (% is in [(FuncHT), (NoFuncHT)])

    1. Return (NoFuncHT)

  3. Case (% is in [(ExnHT), (NoExnHT)])

    1. Return (NoExnHT)

  4. Case (% is in [(ExternHT), (NoExternHT)])

    1. Return (NoExternHT)

1. Else Dangling#761

2. If ((ht matches pattern `DefHT %`)), then

  1. (Let (DefHT dt) be ht)

    1. Return $bot_of_str_type($expanddt(dt))

2. Else Dangling#766

syntax ellipses = 
   | NoEllipses (from ellipses) 
   | Ellipses (from ellipses) 

syntax inferresulttype = (ellipses, valtype*)

syntax inferfunctype = {INS inferresulttype, OUTS inferresulttype}

syntax inferinstrtype = (inferfunctype, localidx*)

var irt : inferresulttype

var ift : inferfunctype

syntax context = {TYPES deftype*, FUNCS deftype*, TABLES tabletype*, MEMS memtype*, TAGS tagtype*, GLOBALS globaltype*, ELEMS elemtype*, DATAS datatype*, LOCALS localtype*, RESULTS valtype*, LABELS resulttype*, REFS free}

var C : context

def $empty_context

1. Return {TYPES [], FUNCS [], TABLES [], MEMS [], TAGS [], GLOBALS [], ELEMS [], DATAS [], LOCALS [], RESULTS [], LABELS [], REFS $free_empty}

relation Match_resulttype: C |- (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} : OK

1. If ((|(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}| = |(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)), then

  1. If ($match_valtype(C.TYPES, t_1, t_2))*{t_1 <- t_1*, t_2 <- t_2*}, then

    1. The relation holds

  1. Else Dangling#771

1. Else Dangling#770

def $stack(resulttype)

1. Return ((NoEllipses), resulttype)

def $pop(C, (ellipses, (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (ellipses', (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}))

1. Case analysis on ellipses

  1. Case (% matches pattern `Ellipses`)

    1. Case analysis on ellipses'

      1. Case (% matches pattern `Ellipses`)

        1. (Let n_1 be |(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)

          1. (Let n_2 be |(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)

            1. (Let n be $min_(n_1, n_2))

              1. (Let int be (n_1 - n))

                1. If ((int has type n)), then

                  1. (Let n_3 be (int as n))

                    1. (Let int' be (n_2 - n))

                      1. If ((int' has type n)), then

                        1. (Let n_4 be (int' as n))

                          1. (Let (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} be (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_4 : n])

                            1. If (Match_resulttype: C |- $make_<valtype>((BotT), n_3) ++ (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK) holds, then

                              1. Return ((Ellipses), [])

                            1. Else Dangling#786

                      1. Else Dangling#783

                1. Else Dangling#780

      2. Case (% matches pattern `NoEllipses`)

        1. (Let n_1 be |(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)

          1. (Let n_2 be |(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)

            1. (Let n be $min_(n_1, n_2))

              1. (Let int be (n_2 - n))

                1. If ((int has type n)), then

                  1. (Let n_3 be (int as n))

                    1. (Let (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} be (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_3 : n])

                      1. If (Match_resulttype: C |- (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK) holds, then

                        1. Return ((NoEllipses), [])

                      1. Else Dangling#795

                1. Else Dangling#792

  2. Case (% matches pattern `NoEllipses`)

    1. Case analysis on ellipses'

      1. Case (% matches pattern `Ellipses`)

        1. (Let n_1 be |(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)

          1. (Let n_2 be |(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)

            1. (Let n be $min_(n_1, n_2))

              1. (Let int be (n_1 - n))

                1. If ((int has type n)), then

                  1. (Let n_3 be (int as n))

                    1. (Let int' be (n_2 - n))

                      1. If ((int' has type n)), then

                        1. (Let n_4 be (int' as n))

                          1. (Let (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} be (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_4 : n])

                            1. If (Match_resulttype: C |- $make_<valtype>((BotT), n_3) ++ (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK) holds, then

                              1. Return ((Ellipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}[0 : n_4])

                            1. Else Dangling#808

                      1. Else Dangling#805

                1. Else Dangling#802

      2. Case (% matches pattern `NoEllipses`)

        1. (Let n_1 be |(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}|)

          1. (Let n_2 be |(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}|)

            1. (Let n be $min_(n_1, n_2))

              1. (Let int be (n_2 - n))

                1. If ((int has type n)), then

                  1. (Let n_3 be (int as n))

                    1. (Let (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} be (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}[n_3 : n])

                      1. If (Match_resulttype: C |- (t_2')*{t_2' <- t_2'*} (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} : OK) holds, then

                        1. Return ((NoEllipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}[0 : n_3])

                      1. Else Dangling#817

                1. Else Dangling#814

def $push(C, (ellipses, (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}), (ellipses', (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}))

1. Case analysis on ellipses

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NoEllipses`)

    1. Case analysis on ellipses'

      1. Case (% matches pattern `NoEllipses`)

        1. Return ((NoEllipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} ++ (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*})

      2. Case (% matches pattern `Ellipses`)

        1. Return ((Ellipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} ++ (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*})

  2. Case (% matches pattern `Ellipses`)

    1. If (((t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} matches pattern [])), then

      1. Return ((Ellipses), (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*})

    1. Else Dangling#823

def $peek(n, (ellipses, (t)*{t <- t*}))

1. If ((n < |(t)*{t <- t*}|)), then

  1. If ((n < |$rev_<valtype>((t)*{t <- t*})|)), then

    1. Return $rev_<valtype>((t)*{t <- t*})[n]

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return (BotT)

def $peek_ref(n, (ellipses, (t)*{t <- t*}))

1. (Let valtype be $peek(n, (ellipses, (t)*{t <- t*})))

  1. If ((valtype matches pattern `RefT %`)), then

    1. (Let (RefT rt) be valtype)

      1. Return rt

  1. Else Dangling#830

2. If (($peek(n, (ellipses, (t)*{t <- t*})) matches pattern `BotT`)), then

  1. Return ((NoNull) (BotHT))

2. Else Dangling#833

def $local(C, x)

1. If ((x < |C.LOCALS|)), then

  1. Return C.LOCALS[x]

1. Else Dangling#835

def $init_local(C, x)

1. (Let (LocalT init t) be $local(C, x))

  1. Return C[LOCALS[x] = (LocalT (Set) t)]

def $init_locals(C, (ti)*{ti <- ti*})

1. Case analysis on (ti)*{ti <- ti*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Return C

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let x_1 :: (x)*{x <- x*} be (ti)*{ti <- ti*})

      1. Return $init_locals($init_local(C, x_1), (x)*{x <- x*})

def $init_valtypes(irt, i, n)

1. If ((i < n)), then

  1. (Let int be (n - i))

    1. If ((int has type n)), then

      1. (Let n' be (int as n))

        1. (Let t be $peek(n', irt))

          1. Return t :: $init_valtypes(irt, (i + 1), n)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return []

relation Instrs_ok: C_0 irt |- (instr')*{instr' <- instr'*} : %

relation Instr_ok: C_0 irt |- instr' : %

1. Case analysis on instr'

  1. Case (% matches pattern `UNREACHABLE`)

    1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((Ellipses), []), OUTS ((Ellipses), [])}, [])

  2. Case (% matches pattern `NOP`)

    1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), []), OUTS ((NoEllipses), [])}, [])

  3. Case (% matches pattern `DROP`)

    1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), [$peek(0, irt)]), OUTS ((NoEllipses), [])}, [])

  4. Case (% matches pattern `SELECT %`)

    1. (Let (SELECT ((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})?{valtype* <- valtype*?}) be instr')

      1. If ((((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})?{valtype* <- valtype*?} matches pattern (_))), then

        1. (Let ?((t)*{t <- t*}) be ((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})?{valtype* <- valtype*?})

          1. If ((|(t)*{t <- t*}| = 1)), then

            1. If (Resulttype_ok: C_0 |- (t)*{t <- t*} : OK) holds, then

              1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), (t)*{t <- t*} ++ (t)*{t <- t*} ++ [(NumT (I32T))]), OUTS ((NoEllipses), (t)*{t <- t*})}, [])

            1. Else Dangling#1009

          1. Else Dangling#1008

      1. Else Dangling#1006

  5. Case (% matches pattern `BLOCK % %`)

    1. (Let (BLOCK blocktype (instr)*{instr <- instr*}) be instr')

      1. (Blocktype_ok: C_0 |- blocktype : (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}))

        1. If (Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}) |- (instr)*{instr <- instr*} : OK) holds, then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}), OUTS ((NoEllipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*})}, (x)*{x <- x*})

        1. Else Dangling#1013

  6. Case (% matches pattern `LOOP % %`)

    1. (Let (LOOP blocktype (instr)*{instr <- instr*}) be instr')

      1. (Blocktype_ok: C_0 |- blocktype : (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}))

        1. If (Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [(t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}) |- (instr)*{instr <- instr*} : OK) holds, then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*}), OUTS ((NoEllipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*})}, (x)*{x <- x*})

        1. Else Dangling#1017

  7. Case (% matches pattern `IF % % ELSE %`)

    1. (Let (IF blocktype (instr_t)*{instr_t <- instr_t*} ELSE (instr_f)*{instr_f <- instr_f*}) be instr')

      1. (Blocktype_ok: C_0 |- blocktype : (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}))

        1. If (Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}) |- (instr_t)*{instr_t <- instr_t*} : OK) holds, then

          1. If (Block_ok: C_0[LABELS = [(t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*}] ++ C_0.LABELS] (InstrT (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*} (x)*{x <- x*}) |- (instr_f)*{instr_f <- instr_f*} : OK) holds, then

            1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), (t_1)*{t_1 <- t_1*} ++ [(NumT (I32T))]), OUTS ((NoEllipses), (t_2)*{t_2 <- t_2*})}, (x)*{x <- x*})

          1. Else Dangling#1022

        1. Else Dangling#1021

  8. Case (% matches pattern `BR %`)

    1. (Let (BR x) be instr')

      1. If ((x < |C_0.LABELS|)), then

        1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((Ellipses), C_0.LABELS[x]), OUTS ((Ellipses), [])}, [])

      1. Else Dangling#1025

  9. Case (% matches pattern `BR_IF %`)

    1. (Let (BR_IF x) be instr')

      1. If ((x < |C_0.LABELS|)), then

        1. (Let (t)*{t <- t*} be C_0.LABELS[x])

          1. Result in: % % |- % : ({INS ((NoEllipses), (t)*{t <- t*} ++ [(NumT (I32T))]), OUTS ((NoEllipses), (t)*{t <- t*})}, [])

      1. Else Dangling#1028

  10. Case (% matches pattern `BR_TABLE % %`)

    1. (Let (BR_TABLE (x)*{x <- x*} y) be instr')

      1. If ((y < |C_0.LABELS|)), then

        1. (Let n be |C_0.LABELS[y]|)

          1. (Let (t)*{t <- t*} be $init_valtypes(irt, 0, n))

            1. If (Match_resulttype: C_0 |- (t)*{t <- t*} C_0.LABELS[y] : OK) holds, then

              1. If ((x < |C_0.LABELS|))*{x <- x*}, then

                1. If (Match_resulttype: C_0 |- (t)*{t <- t*} C_0.LABELS[x] : OK)*{x <- x*} holds, then

    1. (Let export_1 :: (export)*{export <- export*} be (ex)*{ex <- ex*})

      1. (Export_ok: C_0 nameset_0 |- export_1 : nameset_1)

        1. (Exports_ok: C_0 nameset_1 |- (export)*{export <- export*} : nameset_2)

          1. Result in: % % |- % : nameset_2

  2. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Result in: % % |- % : nameset_0

relation Export_ok: C_0 nameset |- {NAME name, EDESC exportdesc} : %

1. Case analysis on exportdesc

  1. Case (% matches pattern `FuncExport %`)

    1. (Let (FuncExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If (((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.FUNCS|))), then

        1. If (~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)), then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : $union_set<name>(nameset, (`{ [name] }))

        1. Else Dangling#1748

      1. Else Dangling#1747

  2. Case (% matches pattern `GlobalExport %`)

    1. (Let (GlobalExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If (((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.GLOBALS|))), then

        1. If (~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)), then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : $union_set<name>(nameset, (`{ [name] }))

        1. Else Dangling#1752

      1. Else Dangling#1751

  3. Case (% matches pattern `TableExport %`)

    1. (Let (TableExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If (((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.TABLES|))), then

        1. If (~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)), then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : $union_set<name>(nameset, (`{ [name] }))

        1. Else Dangling#1756

      1. Else Dangling#1755

  4. Case (% matches pattern `MemExport %`)

    1. (Let (MemExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If (((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.MEMS|))), then

        1. If (~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)), then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : $union_set<name>(nameset, (`{ [name] }))

        1. Else Dangling#1760

      1. Else Dangling#1759

  5. Case (% matches pattern `TagExport %`)

    1. (Let (TagExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If (((x >= 0) /\ (x < |C_0.TAGS|))), then

        1. If (~$in_set<name>(name, nameset)), then

          1. Result in: % % |- % : $union_set<name>(nameset, (`{ [name] }))

        1. Else Dangling#1764

      1. Else Dangling#1763

relation Const_ok: C_0 t |- (instr)*{instr <- instr*} : OK

1. If ($is_const(C_0, instr))*{instr <- instr*}, then

  1. If (Block_ok: C_0 (InstrT [] [t] []) |- (instr)*{instr <- instr*} : OK) holds, then

    1. The relation holds

  1. Else Dangling#1767

1. Else Dangling#1766

relation Module_ok: |- module : OK

1. (Let module' be module[FUNCS = []][START = ?()])

  1. (Let free be $free_module(module'))

    1. (Let C be $empty_context[REFS = free])

      1. (Types_ok: C |- module.TYPES : C_0)

        1. (Imports_ok: C_0 |- module.IMPORTS : C_1)

          1. (Funcs_ok: C_1 |- module.FUNCS : C_2)

            1. (Tables_ok: C_2 |- module.TABLES : C_3)

              1. (Mems_ok: C_3 |- module.MEMS : C_4)

                1. (Globals_ok: C_4 |- module.GLOBALS : C_5)

                  1. (Tags_ok: C_5 |- module.TAGS : C_6)

                    1. (Elems_ok: C_6 |- module.ELEMS : C_7)

                      1. (Datas_ok: C_7 |- module.DATAS : C_8)

                        1. (Let (func)*{func <- func*} be module.FUNCS)

                          1. If (Funcbody_ok: C_8 |- func : OK)*{func <- func*} holds, then

                            1. If (Start_ok: C_8 |- module.START : OK) holds, then

                              1. (Exports_ok: C_8 (`{ [] }) |- module.EXPORTS : nameset)

                                1. The relation holds

                            1. Else Dangling#1783

                          1. Else Dangling#1782

relation Modules_ok: |- (module)*{module <- module*} : OK

1. If (Module_ok: |- module : OK)*{module <- module*} holds, then

  1. The relation holds

1. Else Dangling#1786

2. If (((module)*{module <- module*} matches pattern [])), then

  1. The relation holds

2. Else Dangling#1788

relation Local_ok: C |- {LTYPE t} : %

1. If (Valtype_ok: C |- t : OK) holds, then

  1. Case analysis on $defaultable(t)

    1. Case (% = true)

      1. Result in: % |- % : (LocalT (Set) t)

    2. Case (% = false)

      1. Result in: % |- % : (LocalT (Unset) t)

1. Else Dangling#1790

def $is_const(C, instr)

1. If (instr is in [(BINOP (I32 (IAdd))), (BINOP (I32 (ISub))), (BINOP (I32 (IMul))), (BINOP (I64 (IAdd))), (BINOP (I64 (ISub))), (BINOP (I64 (IMul)))]), then

  1. Return true

2. Case analysis on instr

  1. Case (% matches pattern `CONST %`)

    1. Return true

  2. Case (% matches pattern `VEC.CONST %`)

    1. Return true

  3. Case (% matches pattern `REF.NULL %`)

    1. Return true

  4. Case (% matches pattern `REF.FUNC %`)

    1. Return true

  5. Case (% matches pattern `REF.I31`)

    1. Return true

  6. Case (% matches pattern `STRUCT.NEW % %`)

    1. Return true

  7. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW % %`)

    1. Return true

  8. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW_FIXED % %`)

    1. Return true

  9. Case (% matches pattern `GLOBAL.GET %`)

    1. (Let (GLOBAL.GET x) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |C.GLOBALS|)), then

        1. (Let (GlobalT mut t) be C.GLOBALS[x])

          1. If ((mut matches pattern `Cons`)), then

            1. Return true

3. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

syntax byte = nat

syntax addr = nat

syntax funcaddr = addr

syntax globaladdr = addr

syntax tableaddr = addr

syntax memaddr = addr

syntax tagaddr = addr

syntax elemaddr = addr

syntax dataaddr = addr

syntax hostaddr = addr

syntax structaddr = addr

syntax arrayaddr = addr

syntax exnaddr = addr

syntax externaddr = 
   | FuncAddr funcaddr (from externaddr) 
   | GlobalAddr globaladdr (from externaddr) 
   | TableAddr tableaddr (from externaddr) 
   | MemAddr memaddr (from externaddr) 
   | TagAddr tagaddr (from externaddr) 

syntax refval = 
   | NullRef heaptype (from refval) 
   | FuncRef funcaddr (from refval) 
   | ExternRef refval (from refval) 
   | I31Ref nat (from refval) 
   | StructRef structaddr (from refval) 
   | ArrayRef arrayaddr (from refval) 
   | ExnRef exnaddr (from refval) 
   | HostRef hostaddr (from refval) 

syntax value = 
   | NumV num_ (from value) 
   | VecV vec_ (from value) 
   | RefV refval (from value) 

syntax result = 
   | Values value* (from result) 
   | Trap (from result) 

syntax hostfunc = 
   | HostFuncId hostaddr (from hostfunc) 

syntax funccode = 
   | AstFunc func (from funccode) 
   | HostFunc hostfunc (from funccode) 

syntax exportinst = {NAME name, ADDR externaddr}

syntax moduleinst = {TYPES deftype*, FUNCS funcaddr*, GLOBALS globaladdr*, TABLES tableaddr*, MEMS memaddr*, TAGS tagaddr*, ELEMS elemaddr*, DATAS dataaddr*, EXPORTS exportinst*}

syntax funcinst = {TYPE deftype, MODULE moduleinst, CODE funccode}

syntax globalinst = {TYPE globaltype, VALUE value}

syntax tableinst = {TYPE tabletype, REFS refval*}

syntax meminst = {TYPE memtype, BYTES byte*}

syntax taginst = {TYPE tagtype}

syntax eleminst = {TYPE elemtype, REFS refval*}

syntax datainst = {BYTES byte*}

syntax fieldval = 
   | FieldValue value (from fieldval) 
   | PackedField packtype nat (from fieldval) 

syntax structinst = {TYPE deftype, FIELDS fieldval*}

syntax arrayinst = {TYPE deftype, FIELDS fieldval*}

syntax exninst = {TAG tagaddr, FIELDS value*}

syntax store = {FUNCS funcinst*, GLOBALS globalinst*, TABLES tableinst*, MEMS meminst*, TAGS taginst*, ELEMS eleminst*, DATAS datainst*, STRUCTS structinst*, ARRAYS arrayinst*, EXNS exninst*}

syntax frame = {LOCALS value?*, MODULE moduleinst}

syntax state = (store, frame)

syntax config = (state, instr*)

syntax execresult = 
   | NormalR store frame value* (from execresult) 
   | BreakR store frame nat value* (from execresult) 
   | ReturnR store frame value* (from execresult) 
   | ThrowR store frame tagaddr value* (from execresult)

def $range_from(nat, n)

1. If ((n = 0)), then

  1. Return []

1. Else Dangling#1814

2. If ((n > 0)), then

  1. (Let base be (nat + 1))

    1. (Let int be (n - 1))

      1. If ((int has type n)), then

        1. (Let n' be (int as n))

          1. Return nat :: $range_from(base, n')

      1. Else Dangling#1819

2. Else Dangling#1816

def $limits_min_nat({MIN i64, MAX (_fail64)?{_fail64 <- _fail64?}})

  1. If ((i64 has type n)), then

    1. (Let n be (i64 as n))

      1. Return n

  1. Else Dangling#1823

1. Else Dangling#1822

1. Case analysis on op<i32, i64, f32, f64>

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I32 %`)

    1. (Let (I32 n) be op<i32, i64, f32, f64>)

      1. Return n

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I64 %`)

    1. (Let (I64 fail64) be op<i32, i64, f32, f64>)

      1. If ((fail64 has type i)), then

        1. (Let i be (fail64 as i))

          1. Return i

      1. Else Dangling#1830

1. Else Dangling#1826

def $addr_plus_offset(num, int_1)

1. (Let base be $nat_of_num(num))

  1. If ((int_1 >= (0 as int))), then

    1. If ((int_1 has type nat)), then

      1. (Let offset be (int_1 as nat))

        1. (Let addrval be (base + offset))

          1. Return addrval

    1. Else Dangling#1835

  1. Else Dangling#1834
