def $default_value(valtype)

1. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% = (NumT (I32T)))

    1. Return ?((NumV (I32 0)))

  2. Case (% = (NumT (I64T)))

    1. Return ?((NumV (I64 (0 as i64))))

  3. Case (% = (NumT (F32T)))

    1. Return ?((NumV (F32 (POS (SUBNORM 0)))))

  4. Case (% = (NumT (F64T)))

    1. Return ?((NumV (F64 (POS (SUBNORM 0)))))

  5. Case (% = (VecT (V128T)))

    1. Return ?((VecV (V128 0)))

1. Else Dangling#1889

2. Case analysis on valtype

  1. Case (% matches pattern `RefT %`)

    1. (Let (RefT (null ht)) be valtype)

      1. Case analysis on null

        1. Case (% matches pattern `Null`)

          1. Return ?((RefV (NullRef ht)))

        2. Case (% matches pattern `NoNull`)

          1. Return ?()

  2. Case (% matches pattern `BotT`)

    1. Return ?()

2. Else Dangling#1895

relation Num_ok: s |- op<i32, i64, f32, f64> : %

1. Case analysis on op<i32, i64, f32, f64>

  1. Case (% matches pattern `I32 %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : (I32T)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `I64 %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : (I64T)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `F32 %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : (F32T)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `F64 %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : (F64T)

relation Vec_ok: s |- (V128 _v128) : %

1. Result in: % |- % : (V128T)

relation Ref_ok: s |- r : %

1. Case analysis on r

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NullRef %`)

    1. (Let (NullRef ht) be r)

      1. Result in: % |- % : ((Null) $bot_of_heap_type(ht))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `FuncRef %`)

    1. (Let (FuncRef a) be r)

      1. If ((a < |s.FUNCS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (DefHT s.FUNCS[a].TYPE))

      1. Else Dangling#1911

  3. Case (% matches pattern `I31Ref %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (I31HT))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `StructRef %`)

    1. (Let (StructRef a) be r)

      1. If ((a < |s.STRUCTS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (DefHT s.STRUCTS[a].TYPE))

      1. Else Dangling#1915

  5. Case (% matches pattern `ArrayRef %`)

    1. (Let (ArrayRef a) be r)

      1. If ((a < |s.ARRAYS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (DefHT s.ARRAYS[a].TYPE))

      1. Else Dangling#1918

  6. Case (% matches pattern `ExnRef %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (ExnHT))

  7. Case (% matches pattern `HostRef %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (AnyHT))

  8. Case (% matches pattern `ExternRef %`)

    1. Result in: % |- % : ((NoNull) (ExternHT))

relation Val_ok: s |- v : %

1. Case analysis on v

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NumV %`)

    1. (Let (NumV num) be v)

      1. (Num_ok: s |- num : nt)

        1. Result in: % |- % : (NumT nt)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `VecV %`)

    1. (Let (VecV vec) be v)

      1. (Vec_ok: s |- vec : vt)

        1. Result in: % |- % : (VecT vt)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `RefV %`)

    1. (Let (RefV r) be v)

      1. (Ref_ok: s |- r : rt)

        1. Result in: % |- % : (RefT rt)

def $match_externtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, xt, xt')

1. Case analysis on xt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ExtFunct %`)

    1. (Let (ExtFunct dt_1) be xt)

      1. If ((xt' matches pattern `ExtFunct %`)), then

        1. (Let (ExtFunct dt_2) be xt')

          1. Return $match_deftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2)

  2. Case (% matches pattern `ExtGlobalT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtGlobalT gt_1) be xt)

      1. If ((xt' matches pattern `ExtGlobalT %`)), then

        1. (Let (ExtGlobalT gt_2) be xt')

          1. Return $match_globaltype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, gt_1, gt_2)

  3. Case (% matches pattern `ExtTableT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtTableT tt_1) be xt)

      1. If ((xt' matches pattern `ExtTableT %`)), then

        1. (Let (ExtTableT tt_2) be xt')

          1. Return $match_tabletype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, tt_1, tt_2)

  4. Case (% matches pattern `ExtMemoryT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtMemoryT mt_1) be xt)

      1. If ((xt' matches pattern `ExtMemoryT %`)), then

        1. (Let (ExtMemoryT mt_2) be xt')

          1. Return $match_memtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, mt_1, mt_2)

  5. Case (% matches pattern `ExtTagT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtTagT jt_1) be xt)

      1. If ((xt' matches pattern `ExtTagT %`)), then

        1. (Let (ExtTagT jt_2) be xt')

          1. Return $match_tagtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, jt_1, jt_2)

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

relation Externaddr_ok: s |- xa : %

1. Case analysis on xa

  1. Case (% matches pattern `FuncAddr %`)

    1. (Let (FuncAddr a) be xa)

      1. If ((a < |s.FUNCS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : (ExtFunct s.FUNCS[a].TYPE)

      1. Else Dangling#1957

  2. Case (% matches pattern `GlobalAddr %`)

    1. (Let (GlobalAddr a) be xa)

      1. If ((a < |s.GLOBALS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : (ExtGlobalT s.GLOBALS[a].TYPE)

      1. Else Dangling#1960

  3. Case (% matches pattern `TableAddr %`)

    1. (Let (TableAddr a) be xa)

      1. If ((a < |s.TABLES|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : (ExtTableT s.TABLES[a].TYPE)

      1. Else Dangling#1963

  4. Case (% matches pattern `MemAddr %`)

    1. (Let (MemAddr a) be xa)

      1. If ((a < |s.MEMS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : (ExtMemoryT s.MEMS[a].TYPE)

      1. Else Dangling#1966

  5. Case (% matches pattern `TagAddr %`)

    1. (Let (TagAddr a) be xa)

      1. If ((a < |s.TAGS|)), then

        1. Result in: % |- % : (ExtTagT s.TAGS[a].TYPE)

      1. Else Dangling#1969

def $subst_all_valtype(t, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return $subst_valtype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, t)

def $subst_all_resulttype((t)*{t <- t*}, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return $subst_resulttype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, (t)*{t <- t*})

def $subst_all_heaptype(ht, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return $subst_heaptype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, ht)

def $subst_all_reftype(rt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return $subst_reftype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, rt)

def $subst_all_globaltype((GlobalT mut t), (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return (GlobalT mut $subst_all_valtype(t, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

def $subst_all_tabletype((TableT at lim rt), (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return (TableT at lim $subst_all_reftype(rt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

def $subst_all_memtype(mt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return mt

def $subst_all_tagtype((TagT dt_0), (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return (TagT $subst_all_deftype(dt_0, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

def $subst_all_externtype(xt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Case analysis on xt

  1. Case (% matches pattern `ExtFunct %`)

    1. (Let (ExtFunct dt_0) be xt)

      1. Return (ExtFunct $subst_all_deftype(dt_0, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `ExtTableT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtTableT tt) be xt)

      1. Return (ExtTableT $subst_all_tabletype(tt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

  3. Case (% matches pattern `ExtMemoryT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtMemoryT mt) be xt)

      1. Return (ExtMemoryT $subst_all_memtype(mt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `ExtGlobalT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtGlobalT gt) be xt)

      1. Return (ExtGlobalT $subst_all_globaltype(gt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

  5. Case (% matches pattern `ExtTagT %`)

    1. (Let (ExtTagT jt) be xt)

      1. Return (ExtTagT $subst_all_tagtype(jt, (dt)*{dt <- dt*}))

def $subst_all_deftype(dt_0, (dt)*{dt <- dt*})

1. Return $subst_deftype<deftype*>((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, $subst_of, dt_0)

def $inst_valtype(mm, t)

1. Return $subst_all_valtype(t, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_heaptype(mm, ht)

1. Return $subst_all_heaptype(ht, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_reftype(mm, rt)

1. Return $subst_all_reftype(rt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_globaltype(mm, gt)

1. Return $subst_all_globaltype(gt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_tabletype(mm, tt)

1. Return $subst_all_tabletype(tt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_memtype(mm, mt)

1. Return $subst_all_memtype(mt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_tagtype(mm, jt)

1. Return $subst_all_tagtype(jt, mm.TYPES)

def $inst_externtype(mm, xt)

1. Return $subst_all_externtype(xt, mm.TYPES)

def $deftype_at((deftype)*{deftype <- deftype*}, x)

1. If (((deftype)*{deftype <- deftype*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

  1. (Let dt_h :: (dt_t)*{dt_t <- dt_t*} be (deftype)*{deftype <- deftype*})

    1. If ((x = 0)), then

      1. Return dt_h

    1. Else Dangling#2001

    2. If ((x > 0)), then

      1. (Let int be (x - 1))

        1. If ((int has type idx)), then

          1. (Let x' be (int as idx))

            1. Return $deftype_at((dt_t)*{dt_t <- dt_t*}, x')

        1. Else Dangling#2005

    2. Else Dangling#2003

1. Else Dangling#1999

def $match_limits(lim, lim')

1. (Let {MIN i64_1, MAX (fail64')?{fail64' <- fail64'?}} be lim)

  1. (Let {MIN i64_2, MAX (fail64)?{fail64 <- fail64?}} be lim')

    1. If (((fail64)?{fail64 <- fail64?} matches pattern ())), then

      1. If ($le_u(i64_2, i64_1)), then

        1. Return true

    2. If (((fail64')?{fail64' <- fail64'?} matches pattern (_))), then

      1. (Let ?(i64_1') be (fail64')?{fail64' <- fail64'?})

        1. If (((fail64)?{fail64 <- fail64?} matches pattern (_))), then

          1. (Let ?(i64_2') be (fail64)?{fail64 <- fail64?})

            1. If (($le_u(i64_2, i64_1) /\ $le_u(i64_1', i64_2'))), then

              1. Return true

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_globaltype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, gt, gt')

1. (Let (GlobalT mut t_1) be gt)

  1. (Let (GlobalT mut' t_2) be gt')

    1. Case analysis on mut

      1. Case (% matches pattern `Cons`)

        1. If ((mut' matches pattern `Cons`)), then

          1. Return $match_valtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2)

      2. Case (% matches pattern `Var`)

        1. If ((mut' matches pattern `Var`)), then

          1. Return ($match_valtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, t_1, t_2) /\ $match_valtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, t_2, t_1))

2. Otherwise,

  1. Return false

def $match_tabletype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (TableT at_1 lim_1 rt_1), (TableT at_2 lim_2 rt_2))

1. Return ((((at_1 = at_2) /\ $match_limits(lim_1, lim_2)) /\ $match_reftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, rt_1, rt_2)) /\ $match_reftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, rt_2, rt_1))

def $match_memtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (MemoryT at_1 lim_1), (MemoryT at_2 lim_2))

1. Return ((at_1 = at_2) /\ $match_limits(lim_1, lim_2))

def $match_tagtype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, (TagT dt_1), (TagT dt_2))

1. Return ($match_deftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, dt_1, dt_2) /\ $match_deftype((dt)*{dt <- dt*}, dt_2, dt_1))

relation Eval_const: s mm |- const : %

1. (Exec_instrs: s mm [] |- const : (s', (v'')*{v'' <- v''*}))

  1. If (((v'')*{v'' <- v''*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

    1. (Let v :: (v')*{v' <- v'*} be (v'')*{v'' <- v''*})

      1. If (((v')*{v' <- v'*} matches pattern [])), then

        1. Result in: % % |- % : (s', v)

      1. Else Dangling#2034

  1. Else Dangling#2032

relation Eval_consts: s mm |- (const')*{const' <- const'*} : %

1. Case analysis on (const')*{const' <- const'*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let const_1 :: (const)*{const <- const*} be (const')*{const' <- const'*})

      1. (Eval_const: s mm |- const_1 : (s_1, v_1))

        1. (Eval_consts: s_1 mm |- (const)*{const <- const*} : (s_2, (v)*{v <- v*}))

          1. Result in: % % |- % : (s_2, v_1 :: (v)*{v <- v*})

  2. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Result in: % % |- % : (s, [])

relation Exec_instr: s mm (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*} |- instr : %

1. Case analysis on instr

  1. Case (% matches pattern `NOP`)

    1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

  2. Case (% matches pattern `CONST %`)

    1. (Let (CONST num) be instr)

      1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (NumV num) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

  3. Case (% matches pattern `VEC.CONST %`)

    1. (Let (VEC.CONST vec) be instr)

      1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (VecV vec) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

  4. Case (% matches pattern `REF.NULL %`)

    1. (Let (REF.NULL ht) be instr)

      1. (Let ht' be $inst_heaptype(mm, ht))

        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (RefV (NullRef ht')) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

  5. Case (% matches pattern `REF.FUNC %`)

    1. (Let (REF.FUNC x) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |mm.FUNCS|)), then

        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (RefV (FuncRef mm.FUNCS[x])) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

      1. Else Dangling#2052

  6. Case (% matches pattern `GLOBAL.GET %`)

    1. (Let (GLOBAL.GET x) be instr)

      1. If ((mm.GLOBALS[x] < |s.GLOBALS|)), then

        1. If ((x < |mm.GLOBALS|)), then

          1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, s.GLOBALS[mm.GLOBALS[x]].VALUE :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

        1. Else Dangling#2056

      1. Else Dangling#2055

  7. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.SIZE %`)

    1. (Let (TABLE.SIZE x) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |mm.TABLES|)), then

        1. (Let ta be mm.TABLES[x])

          1. If ((ta < |s.TABLES|)), then

            1. (Let (TableT at lim rt) be s.TABLES[ta].TYPE)

              1. Case analysis on at

                1. Case (% matches pattern `I32AT`)

                  1. (Let n be |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)

                    1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (NumV (I32 n)) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

                2. Case (% matches pattern `I64AT`)

                  1. (Let n be |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)

                    1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (NumV (I64 (n as i64))) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

          1. Else Dangling#2061

      1. Else Dangling#2059

  8. Case (% matches pattern `ELEM.DROP %`)

    1. (Let (ELEM.DROP x) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |mm.ELEMS|)), then

        1. (Let ea be mm.ELEMS[x])

          1. (Let s' be s[ELEMS[ea].REFS = []])

            1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

      1. Else Dangling#2069

  9. Case (% matches pattern `DATA.DROP %`)

    1. (Let (DATA.DROP x) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |mm.DATAS|)), then

        1. (Let da be mm.DATAS[x])

          1. (Let s' be s[DATAS[da].BYTES = []])

            1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

      1. Else Dangling#2074

  10. Case (% matches pattern `CALL %`)

    1. (Let (CALL x) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |mm.FUNCS|)), then

        1. (Let fa be mm.FUNCS[x])

          1. (Invoke_funcaddr: s (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*} |- fa : (s', (v)*{v <- v*}))

            1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (v)*{v <- v*})

      1. Else Dangling#2079

  11. Case (% matches pattern `STRUCT.NEW % %`)

    1. (Let (STRUCT.NEW x iop) be instr)

      1. Case analysis on iop

        1. Case (% matches pattern `Explicit`)

          1. If ((x < |mm.TYPES|)), then

            1. (Let strt be $expanddt(mm.TYPES[x]))

              1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefStructT %`)), then

                1. (Let (DefStructT (StructT (yt)*{yt <- yt*})) be strt)

                  1. (Let n be |(yt)*{yt <- yt*}|)

                    1. (Let (v_taken)*{v_taken <- v_taken*} be $take_<value>((v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*}, n))

                      1. (Let (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} be $rev_<value>((v_taken)*{v_taken <- v_taken*}))

                        1. (Let int be (|(v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*}| - n))

                          1. If ((int has type nat)), then

                            1. (Let nrest be (int as nat))

                              1. (Let (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} be (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*}[n : nrest])

                                1. (Let (fv)*{fv <- fv*} be $pack_fields((yt)*{yt <- yt*}, (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*}))

                                  1. (Let si be {TYPE mm.TYPES[x], FIELDS (fv)*{fv <- fv*}})

                                    1. (Let a be |s.STRUCTS|)

                                      1. (Let s' be s[STRUCTS = s.STRUCTS ++ [si]])

                                        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (RefV (StructRef a)) :: (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                          1. Else Dangling#2093

              1. Else Dangling#2087

          1. Else Dangling#2085

        2. Case (% matches pattern `Implicit`)

          1. If ((x < |mm.TYPES|)), then

            1. (Let strt be $expanddt(mm.TYPES[x]))

              1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefStructT %`)), then

                1. (Let (DefStructT (StructT (yt)*{yt <- yt*})) be strt)

                  1. (Let (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} be $default_field_values((yt)*{yt <- yt*}))

                    1. (Let (fv)*{fv <- fv*} be $pack_fields((yt)*{yt <- yt*}, (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*}))

                      1. (Let si be {TYPE mm.TYPES[x], FIELDS (fv)*{fv <- fv*}})

                        1. (Let a be |s.STRUCTS|)

                          1. (Let s' be s[STRUCTS = s.STRUCTS ++ [si]])

                            1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (RefV (StructRef a)) :: (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

              1. Else Dangling#2103

          1. Else Dangling#2101

  12. Case (% matches pattern `ARRAY.NEW_FIXED % %`)

    1. (Let (ARRAY.NEW_FIXED x n) be instr)

      1. If ((x < |mm.TYPES|)), then

        1. (Let strt be $expanddt(mm.TYPES[x]))

          1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefArrayT %`)), then

            1. (Let (DefArrayT (ArrayT (FieldT mut zt))) be strt)

              1. (Let (v_taken)*{v_taken <- v_taken*} be $take_<value>((v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*}, n))

                1. (Let (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} be $rev_<value>((v_taken)*{v_taken <- v_taken*}))

                  1. (Let int be (|(v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*}| - n))

                    1. If ((int has type nat)), then

                      1. (Let nrest be (int as nat))

                        1. (Let (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} be (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*}[n : nrest])

                          1. (Let (fv)*{fv <- fv*} be ($pack_field(zt, v_arg))*{v_arg <- v_arg*})

                            1. (Let ai be {TYPE mm.TYPES[x], FIELDS (fv)*{fv <- fv*}})

                              1. (Let a be |s.ARRAYS|)

                                1. (Let s' be s[ARRAYS = s.ARRAYS ++ [ai]])

                                  1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (RefV (ArrayRef a)) :: (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                    1. Else Dangling#2119

          1. Else Dangling#2114

      1. Else Dangling#2112

1. Else Dangling#2042

2. If (((v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

  1. (Let v_1 :: (v)*{v <- v*} be (v''''')*{v''''' <- v'''''*})

    1. If ((instr matches pattern `DROP`)), then

      1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, (v)*{v <- v*})

    1. Else Dangling#2129

    2. If (((v)*{v <- v*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

      1. (Let v_2 :: (v')*{v' <- v'*} be (v)*{v <- v*})

        1. If (((v')*{v' <- v'*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

          1. (Let v_1 :: (v)*{v <- v*} be (v')*{v' <- v'*})

            1. Case analysis on v_1

              1. Case (% matches pattern `NumV %`)

                1. (Let (NumV op<i32, i64, f32, f64>) be v_1)

                  1. If ((op<i32, i64, f32, f64> matches pattern `I32 %`)), then

                    1. (Let (I32 i) be op<i32, i64, f32, f64>)

                      1. If ((instr matches pattern `SELECT %`)), then

                        1. If ((i =/= 0)), then

                          1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, v_1 :: (v)*{v <- v*})

                        1. Else Dangling#2140

                        2. If ((i = 0)), then

                          1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s, v_2 :: (v)*{v <- v*})

                        2. Else Dangling#2142

                      1. Else Dangling#2139

                  1. Else Dangling#2137

                  2. Case analysis on v_2

                    1. Case (% matches pattern `RefV %`)

                      1. (Let (RefV r) be v_2)

                        1. If ((v_1 matches pattern `NumV %`)), then

                          1. (Let (NumV num_n) be v_1)

                            1. If ((instr matches pattern `TABLE.FILL %`)), then

                              1. (Let (TABLE.FILL x) be instr)

                                1. If ((x < |mm.TABLES|)), then

                                  1. (Let ta be mm.TABLES[x])

                                    1. (Let offset be $nat_of_num(op<i32, i64, f32, f64>))

                                      1. (Let n be $nat_of_num(num_n))

                                        1. If ((ta < |s.TABLES|)), then

                                          1. If (((offset + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)), then

                                            1. (Let s' be s[TABLES[ta].REFS = $replace_slice_<refval>(s.TABLES[ta].REFS, offset, $make_<refval>(r, n))])

                                              1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (v)*{v <- v*})

                                          1. Else Dangling#2155

                                        1. Else Dangling#2154

                                1. Else Dangling#2150

                            1. Else Dangling#2148

                        1. Else Dangling#2146

                    2. Case (% matches pattern `NumV %`)

                      1. (Let (NumV num_s) be v_2)

                        1. If ((v_1 matches pattern `NumV %`)), then

                          1. (Let (NumV num_n) be v_1)

                            1. Case analysis on instr

                              1. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.COPY % %`)

                                1. (Let (TABLE.COPY x y) be instr)

                                  1. If ((x < |mm.TABLES|)), then

                                    1. (Let ta be mm.TABLES[x])

                                      1. If ((y < |mm.TABLES|)), then

                                        1. (Let ta' be mm.TABLES[y])

                                          1. (Let n be $nat_of_num(num_n))

                                            1. (Let offset be $nat_of_num(op<i32, i64, f32, f64>))

                                              1. (Let src be $nat_of_num(num_s))

                                                1. If ((ta < |s.TABLES|)), then

                                                  1. If (((offset + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)), then

                                                    1. If ((ta' < |s.TABLES|)), then

                                                      1. If (((src + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta'].REFS|)), then

                                                        1. (Let (r)*{r <- r*} be s.TABLES[ta'].REFS[src : n])

                                                          1. (Let s' be s[TABLES[ta].REFS = $replace_slice_<refval>(s.TABLES[ta].REFS, offset, (r)*{r <- r*})])

                                                            1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (v)*{v <- v*})

                                                      1. Else Dangling#2173

                                                    1. Else Dangling#2172

                                                  1. Else Dangling#2171

                                                1. Else Dangling#2170

                                      1. Else Dangling#2165

                                  1. Else Dangling#2163

                              2. Case (% matches pattern `TABLE.INIT % %`)

                                1. (Let (TABLE.INIT x y) be instr)

                                  1. If ((x < |mm.TABLES|)), then

                                    1. (Let ta be mm.TABLES[x])

                                      1. If ((y < |mm.ELEMS|)), then

                                        1. (Let ea be mm.ELEMS[y])

                                          1. (Let n be $nat_of_num(num_n))

                                            1. (Let offset be $nat_of_num(op<i32, i64, f32, f64>))

                                              1. (Let src be $nat_of_num(num_s))

                                                1. If ((ta < |s.TABLES|)), then

                                                  1. If (((offset + n) <= |s.TABLES[ta].REFS|)), then

                                                    1. If ((ea < |s.ELEMS|)), then

                                                      1. If (((src + n) <= |s.ELEMS[ea].REFS|)), then

                                                        1. (Let (r)*{r <- r*} be s.ELEMS[ea].REFS[src : n])

                                                          1. (Let s' be s[TABLES[ta].REFS = $replace_slice_<refval>(s.TABLES[ta].REFS, offset, (r)*{r <- r*})])

                                                            1. Result in: % % % |- % : (s', (v)*{v <- v*})

                                                      1. Else Dangling#2188

                                                    1. Else Dangling#2187

                                                  1. Else Dangling#2186

                                                1. Else Dangling#2185

                                      1. Else Dangling#2180

                                  1. Else Dangling#2178

                              3. Case (% matches pattern `MEMORY.COPY % %`)

                                1. (Let (MEMORY.COPY x y) be instr)

                                  1. If ((x < |mm.MEMS|)), then

                                    1. (Let ma be mm.MEMS[x])

                                      1. If ((y < |mm.MEMS|)), then

                                        1. (Let ma' be mm.MEMS[y])

                                          1. (Let n be $nat_of_num(num_n))

                                            1. (Let offset be $nat_of_num(op<i32, i64, f32, f64>))

                                              1. (Let src be $nat_of_num(num_s))

                                                1. If ((ma < |s.MEMS|)), then

                                                  1. If (((offset + n) <= |s.MEMS[ma].BYTES|)), then

                                                    1. If ((ma' < |s.MEMS|)), then

relation Exec_instr_ctrl: s fr (v''')*{v''' <- v'''*} |- instr' : %

              1. If ((er matches pattern `NormalR % % %`)), then

                1. (Let (NormalR s' frp (v_label)*{v_label <- v_label*}) be er)

                    1. Result in: % % % |- % : (NormalR s' frp (v)*{v <- v*})

              1. Else Dangling#2962

        2. (Let nresult be $block_result_arity(fr.MODULE, blocktype))

          1. (Let (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} be $block_input_stack((v''')*{v''' <- v'''*}, nparam))

            1. (Let (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} be $block_outer_stack((v''')*{v''' <- v'''*}, nparam))

              1. (Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} |- (instr)*{instr <- instr*} : er)

                  1. (Let (BreakR s' frp nat (v_break)*{v_break <- v_break*}) be er)

                    1. If ((nat = 0)), then

                        1. (Let (v)*{v <- v*} be (v_take)*{v_take <- v_take*} ++ (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                    1. Else Dangling#2972

              1. Case (% matches pattern `BreakR % % % %`)

                1. (Let (BreakR s' frp l (v)*{v <- v*}) be er)

          1. Else Dangling#3204

          2. Case analysis on instr'

            1. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_NULL %`)

              1. (Ref_ok: s |- r : (null ht))

                1. If ((null matches pattern `NoNull`)), then

                  1. Result in: % % % |- % : (NormalR s fr (RefV r) :: (v)*{v <- v*})

                1. Else Dangling#3228

            2. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_NON_NULL %`)

              1. (Let (BR_ON_NON_NULL l) be instr')

                1. (Ref_ok: s |- r : (null ht))

                  1. If ((null matches pattern `NoNull`)), then

                    1. Result in: % % % |- % : (BreakR s fr l (RefV r) :: (v)*{v <- v*})

                  1. Else Dangling#3232

            3. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_CAST % % %`)

              1. (Let (BR_ON_CAST l rt_1 rt_2) be instr')

                1. (Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual)

                  1. (Let rt_expect be $inst_reftype(fr.MODULE, rt_2))

                    1. Case analysis on $match_reftype(fr.MODULE.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

                      1. Case true

                        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (BreakR s fr l (RefV r) :: (v)*{v <- v*})

                      2. Case false

                        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (NormalR s fr (RefV r) :: (v)*{v <- v*})

            4. Case (% matches pattern `BR_ON_CAST_FAIL % % %`)

              1. (Let (BR_ON_CAST_FAIL l rt_1 rt_2) be instr')

                1. (Ref_ok: s |- r : rtactual)

                  1. (Let rt_expect be $inst_reftype(fr.MODULE, rt_2))

                    1. Case analysis on $match_reftype(fr.MODULE.TYPES, rtactual, rt_expect)

                      1. Case true

                        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (NormalR s fr (RefV r) :: (v)*{v <- v*})

                      2. Case false

                        1. Result in: % % % |- % : (BreakR s fr l (RefV r) :: (v)*{v <- v*})

          2. Else Dangling#3226

    1. Else Dangling#3084

2. Else Dangling#3082

3. (Exec_instr_frame: s fr (v''')*{v''' <- v'''*} |- instr' : (s', frp, (v)*{v <- v*}))

  1. Result in: % % % |- % : (NormalR s' frp (v)*{v <- v*})

relation Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr (v)*{v <- v*} |- (instr')*{instr' <- instr'*} : %

1. Case analysis on (instr')*{instr' <- instr'*}

  1. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let instr_1 :: (instr)*{instr <- instr*} be (instr')*{instr' <- instr'*})

      1. (Exec_instr_ctrl: s fr (v)*{v <- v*} |- instr_1 : er')

        1. Case analysis on er'

          1. Case (% matches pattern `NormalR % % %`)

            1. (Let (NormalR s_1 fr_1 (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*}) be er')

              1. (Exec_instrs_ctrl: s_1 fr_1 (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*} |- (instr)*{instr <- instr*} : er)

                1. Result in: % % % |- % : er

          2. Case (% matches pattern `BreakR % % % %`)

            1. (Let (BreakR s_1 fr_1 l (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*}) be er')

              1. Result in: % % % |- % : (BreakR s_1 fr_1 l (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*})

          3. Case (% matches pattern `ReturnR % % %`)

            1. (Let (ReturnR s_1 fr_1 (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*}) be er')

              1. Result in: % % % |- % : (ReturnR s_1 fr_1 (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*})

          4. Case (% matches pattern `ThrowR % % % %`)

            1. (Let (ThrowR s_1 fr_1 ja (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*}) be er')

              1. Result in: % % % |- % : (ThrowR s_1 fr_1 ja (v_1)*{v_1 <- v_1*})

  2. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. Result in: % % % |- % : (NormalR s fr (v)*{v <- v*})

relation Invoke_funcaddr: s (v)*{v <- v*} |- fa : %

1. If ((fa < |s.FUNCS|)), then

  1. (Let fi be s.FUNCS[fa])

    1. (Let funccode be fi.CODE)

      1. Case analysis on funccode

        1. Case (% matches pattern `AstFunc %`)

          1. (Let (AstFunc func) be funccode)

            1. (Let strt be $expanddt(fi.TYPE))

              1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)), then

                1. (Let (DefFuncT (FuncT (t_arg)*{t_arg <- t_arg*} (t_result)*{t_result <- t_result*})) be strt)

                  1. (Let n be |(t_arg)*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|)

                    1. (Let (v_arg_stack)*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*} be $take_<value>((v)*{v <- v*}, n))

                      1. (Let (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} be $rev_<value>((v_arg_stack)*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*}))

                        1. (Let int be (|(v)*{v <- v*}| - n))

                          1. If ((int has type nat)), then

                            1. (Let nrest be (int as nat))

                              1. (Let (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} be (v)*{v <- v*}[n : nrest])

                                1. (Let fr be {LOCALS $some_values((v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*}) ++ $default_locals(fi.MODULE, func.LOCALS), MODULE fi.MODULE})

                                  1. (Exec_instrs_ctrl: s fr [] |- func.BODY : er)

                                    1. Case analysis on er

                                      1. Case (% matches pattern `NormalR % % %`)

                                        1. (Let (NormalR s' frp (v_result_stack)*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}) be er)

                                          1. (Let m be |(t_result)*{t_result <- t_result*}|)

                                            1. (Let (v_results)*{v_results <- v_results*} be $take_<value>((v_result_stack)*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}, m))

                                              1. (Let (v')*{v' <- v'*} be (v_results)*{v_results <- v_results*} ++ (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                                                1. Result in: % % |- % : (s', (v')*{v' <- v'*})

                                      2. Case (% matches pattern `ReturnR % % %`)

                                        1. (Let (ReturnR s' frp (v_result_stack)*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}) be er)

                                          1. (Let m be |(t_result)*{t_result <- t_result*}|)

                                            1. (Let (v_results)*{v_results <- v_results*} be $take_<value>((v_result_stack)*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}, m))

                                              1. (Let (v')*{v' <- v'*} be (v_results)*{v_results <- v_results*} ++ (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                                                1. Result in: % % |- % : (s', (v')*{v' <- v'*})

                                      3. Case (% matches pattern `BreakR % % % %`)

                                        1. (Let (BreakR s' frp nat (v_result_stack)*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}) be er)

                                          1. If ((nat = 0)), then

                                            1. (Let m be |(t_result)*{t_result <- t_result*}|)

                                              1. (Let (v_results)*{v_results <- v_results*} be $take_<value>((v_result_stack)*{v_result_stack <- v_result_stack*}, m))

                                                1. (Let (v')*{v' <- v'*} be (v_results)*{v_results <- v_results*} ++ (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                                                  1. Result in: % % |- % : (s', (v')*{v' <- v'*})

                                          1. Else Dangling#3291

                                    1. Else Dangling#3279

                          1. Else Dangling#3274

              1. Else Dangling#3268

        2. Case (% matches pattern `HostFunc %`)

          1. (Let strt be $expanddt(fi.TYPE))

            1. If ((strt matches pattern `DefFuncT %`)), then

              1. (Let (DefFuncT (FuncT (t_arg)*{t_arg <- t_arg*} (valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*})) be strt)

                1. If (((valtype)*{valtype <- valtype*} matches pattern [])), then

                  1. If ((|(t_arg)*{t_arg <- t_arg*}| <= |(v)*{v <- v*}|)), then

                    1. (Let int be (|(v)*{v <- v*}| - |(t_arg)*{t_arg <- t_arg*}|))

                      1. If ((int has type n)), then

                        1. (Let n be (int as n))

                          1. (Let (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} be (v)*{v <- v*}[|(t_arg)*{t_arg <- t_arg*}| : n])

                            1. Result in: % % |- % : (s, (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                      1. Else Dangling#3302

                  1. Else Dangling#3300

                1. Else Dangling#3299

            1. Else Dangling#3297

1. Else Dangling#3262

relation Invoke_funcaddr_throw: s (v)*{v <- v*} |- fa : %

1. If ((fa < |s.FUNCS|)), then

    1. (Let funccode be fi.CODE)

                    1. (Let (v_arg)*{v_arg <- v_arg*} be $rev_<value>((v_arg_stack)*{v_arg_stack <- v_arg_stack*}))

            1. Else Dangling#3312

      1. Else Dangling#3309

relation Handle_throw: s fr (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- (catch'')*{catch'' <- catch''*} : %

    1. (Let catch' :: (catch)*{catch <- catch*} be (catch'')*{catch'' <- catch''*})

          1. (Let (Catch x y) be catch')

                1. Case true

                  1. Result in: % % % % % |- % : (BreakR s fr y (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*} ++ (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                2. Case false

                  1. (Handle_throw: s fr (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- (catch)*{catch <- catch*} : er)

                    1. Result in: % % % % % |- % : er

            1. Else Dangling#3330

        2. Case (% matches pattern `CatchRef % %`)

          1. (Let (CatchRef x y) be catch')

            1. If ((x < |fr.MODULE.TAGS|)), then

              1. Case analysis on (ja = fr.MODULE.TAGS[x])

                1. Case true

                  1. (Let a be |s.EXNS|)

                    1. (Let s' be s[EXNS = s.EXNS ++ [{TAG ja, FIELDS (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*}}]])

                      1. Result in: % % % % % |- % : (BreakR s' fr y (RefV (ExnRef a)) :: (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*} ++ (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

                2. Case false

                  1. (Handle_throw: s fr (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*} ja (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*} |- (catch)*{catch <- catch*} : er)

                    1. Result in: % % % % % |- % : er

            1. Else Dangling#3336

        3. Case (% matches pattern `CatchAll %`)

          1. (Let (CatchAll x) be catch')

            1. Result in: % % % % % |- % : (BreakR s fr x (v_rest)*{v_rest <- v_rest*})

    1. Result in: % % % % % |- % : (ThrowR s fr ja (v_throw)*{v_throw <- v_throw*})

def $pack_field(zt, v)

  1. Return (FieldValue v)

1. Else Dangling#3356

  1. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I8)))

      1. (Let (NumV op<i32, i64, f32, f64>) be v)

        1. If ((op<i32, i64, f32, f64> matches pattern `I32 %`)), then

          1. (Let (I32 n) be op<i32, i64, f32, f64>)

            1. Return (PackedField (I8) $byte_of_i32(n))

        1. Else Dangling#3361

    1. Else Dangling#3359

  2. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I16)))

    1. If ((v matches pattern `NumV %`)), then

      1. (Let (NumV op<i32, i64, f32, f64>) be v)

        1. If ((op<i32, i64, f32, f64> matches pattern `I32 %`)), then

          1. (Let (I32 n) be op<i32, i64, f32, f64>)

            1. Return (PackedField (I16) $i32_of_bytes($bytes_of_i32(n)[0 : 2]))

        1. Else Dangling#3366

    1. Else Dangling#3364

2. Else Dangling#3358

def $storage_size_nat(zt)

1. Case analysis on zt

  1. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I8)))

    1. Return 1

  2. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I16)))

    1. Return 2

  3. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I32)))

    1. Return 4

  4. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I64)))

    1. Return 8

  5. Case (% = (ValStorageT (NumT (I32T))))

    1. Return 4

  6. Case (% = (ValStorageT (NumT (I64T))))

    1. Return 8

  7. Case (% = (ValStorageT (NumT (F32T))))

    1. Return 4

  8. Case (% = (ValStorageT (NumT (F64T))))

    1. Return 8

  9. Case (% = (ValStorageT (VecT (V128T))))

    1. Return 16

1. Else Dangling#3407

def $fieldval_of_storage_bytes(zt, (b)*{b <- b*})

  1. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I8)))

    1. If ((0 < |(b)*{b <- b*}|)), then

      1. Return (PackedField (I8) (b)*{b <- b*}[0])

    1. Else Dangling#3418

  2. Case (% = (PackStorageT (I16)))

    1. Return (PackedField (I16) $i32_of_bytes((b)*{b <- b*}))

  3. Case (% = (ValStorageT (NumT (I32T))))

    1. Return (FieldValue (NumV (I32 $i32_of_bytes((b)*{b <- b*}))))

  4. Case (% = (ValStorageT (NumT (I64T))))

    1. Return (FieldValue (NumV (I64 ($i64_of_bytes((b)*{b <- b*}) as i64))))

1. Else Dangling#3417

  2. Case (% matches pattern _ :: _)

    1. (Let type_1 :: (type)*{type <- type*} be (ty)*{ty <- ty*})

              1. Return $alloctypes_from((dt_all)*{dt_all <- dt_all*}, (type)*{type <- type*})

def $allocexport(mm, {NAME name, EDESC exportdesc})

1. Case analysis on exportdesc

  1. Case (% matches pattern `FuncExport %`)

    1. (Let (FuncExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If ((x < |mm.FUNCS|)), then

        1. Return {NAME name, ADDR (FuncAddr mm.FUNCS[x])}

      1. Else Dangling#3558

  2. Case (% matches pattern `GlobalExport %`)

    1. (Let (GlobalExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If ((x < |mm.GLOBALS|)), then

        1. Return {NAME name, ADDR (GlobalAddr mm.GLOBALS[x])}

      1. Else Dangling#3561

  3. Case (% matches pattern `TableExport %`)

    1. (Let (TableExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If ((x < |mm.TABLES|)), then

        1. Return {NAME name, ADDR (TableAddr mm.TABLES[x])}

      1. Else Dangling#3564

  4. Case (% matches pattern `MemExport %`)

    1. (Let (MemExport x) be exportdesc)

      1. If ((x < |mm.MEMS|)), then

        1. Return {NAME name, ADDR (MemAddr mm.MEMS[x])}

      1. Else Dangling#3567

  5. Case (% matches pattern `TagExport %`)

      1. If ((x < |mm.TAGS|)), then

      1. Else Dangling#3570

def $import_externtype(mm, {MODULENAME _name, ITEMNAME _name', IDESC importdesc})

1. Case analysis on importdesc

  1. Case (% matches pattern `FuncImport %`)

    1. (Let (FuncImport x) be importdesc)

      1. Return (ExtFunct $deftype_at(mm.TYPES, x))

  2. Case (% matches pattern `GlobalImport %`)

    1. (Let (GlobalImport gt) be importdesc)

      1. Return (ExtGlobalT $inst_globaltype(mm, gt))

  3. Case (% matches pattern `TableImport %`)

    1. (Let (TableImport tt) be importdesc)

      1. Return (ExtTableT $inst_tabletype(mm, tt))

  4. Case (% matches pattern `MemImport %`)

    1. (Let (MemImport mt) be importdesc)

      1. Return (ExtMemoryT $inst_memtype(mm, mt))

  5. Case (% matches pattern `TagImport %`)

    1. (Let (TagImport x) be importdesc)

      1. Return (ExtTagT (TagT $deftype_at(mm.TYPES, x)))

relation Import_match_ok: s mm |- xa import : OK

1. (Externaddr_ok: s |- xa : xt_actual)

  1. (Let xt_expect be $import_externtype(mm, import))

    1. If ($match_externtype(mm.TYPES, xt_actual, xt_expect)), then

      1. The relation holds

    1. Else Dangling#3586

    1. (Let xa :: (xa_t)*{xa_t <- xa_t*} be (externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*})

      1. If (((import)*{import <- import*} matches pattern _ :: _)), then

        1. (Let import_1 :: (import_t)*{import_t <- import_t*} be (import)*{import <- import*})

          1. If (Import_match_ok: s mm |- xa import_1 : OK) holds, then

            1. If (Imports_match_ok: s mm |- (xa_t)*{xa_t <- xa_t*} (import_t)*{import_t <- import_t*} : OK) holds, then

              1. The relation holds

            1. Else Dangling#3593

          1. Else Dangling#3592

      1. Else Dangling#3590

  2. Case (% matches pattern [])

    1. If (((import)*{import <- import*} matches pattern [])), then

      1. The relation holds

    1. Else Dangling#3595

relation Init_allocated: s_0 module (externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*} |- % : %

1. If (Module_ok: |- module : OK) holds, then

  1. (Let (dt)*{dt <- dt*} be $alloctypes(module.TYPES))

    1. (Let (fa_import)*{fa_import <- fa_import*} be $externaddr_funcaddr((externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*}))

      1. (Let (ga_import)*{ga_import <- ga_import*} be $externaddr_globaladdr((externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*}))

        1. (Let (ta_import)*{ta_import <- ta_import*} be $externaddr_tableaddr((externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*}))

          1. (Let (ma_import)*{ma_import <- ma_import*} be $externaddr_memaddr((externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*}))

            1. (Let (ja_import)*{ja_import <- ja_import*} be $externaddr_tagaddr((externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*}))

              1. (Let (fa)*{fa <- fa*} be (fa_import)*{fa_import <- fa_import*} ++ $range_from(|s_0.FUNCS|, |module.FUNCS|))

                1. (Let (ga)*{ga <- ga*} be (ga_import)*{ga_import <- ga_import*} ++ $range_from(|s_0.GLOBALS|, |module.GLOBALS|))

                  1. (Let (ta)*{ta <- ta*} be (ta_import)*{ta_import <- ta_import*} ++ $range_from(|s_0.TABLES|, |module.TABLES|))

                    1. (Let (ma)*{ma <- ma*} be (ma_import)*{ma_import <- ma_import*} ++ $range_from(|s_0.MEMS|, |module.MEMS|))

                      1. (Let (ja)*{ja <- ja*} be (ja_import)*{ja_import <- ja_import*} ++ $range_from(|s_0.TAGS|, |module.TAGS|))

                        1. (Let (ea)*{ea <- ea*} be $range_from(|s_0.ELEMS|, |module.ELEMS|))

                          1. (Let (da)*{da <- da*} be $range_from(|s_0.DATAS|, |module.DATAS|))

                            1. (Let mmbase be {TYPES (dt)*{dt <- dt*}, FUNCS (fa)*{fa <- fa*}, GLOBALS (ga)*{ga <- ga*}, TABLES (ta)*{ta <- ta*}, MEMS (ma)*{ma <- ma*}, TAGS (ja)*{ja <- ja*}, ELEMS (ea)*{ea <- ea*}, DATAS (da)*{da <- da*}, EXPORTS []})

                              1. If (Imports_match_ok: s_0 mmbase |- (externaddr)*{externaddr <- externaddr*} module.IMPORTS : OK) holds, then

                                1. (Let (xi)*{xi <- xi*} be $allocexports(mmbase, module.EXPORTS))

                                  1. (Let mm be mmbase[EXPORTS = (xi)*{xi <- xi*}])

                                    1. (Alloc_funcs: s_0 mm (dt)*{dt <- dt*} |- module.FUNCS : s_1)

                                      1. (Alloc_globals: s_1 mm |- module.GLOBALS : s_2)

                                        1. (Alloc_tables: s_2 mm |- module.TABLES : s_3)

                                          1. (Alloc_mems: s_3 mm |- module.MEMS : s_4)

                                            1. (Alloc_tags: s_4 mm (dt)*{dt <- dt*} |- module.TAGS : s_5)

                                              1. (Alloc_elems: s_5 mm |- module.ELEMS : s_6)

                                                1. (Alloc_datas: s_6 mm |- module.DATAS : s_7)

                                                  1. Result in: % % % |- mm : s_7

                              1. Else Dangling#3728

1. Else Dangling#3713
